### [1] [Sol Video Inference Engine: Agent-Native Full-Stack Acceleration Framework for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23743)
*Yitong Li,Junsong Chen,Haopeng Li,Haozhe Liu,Jincheng Yu,Ligeng Zhu,Ping Luo,Song Han,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Sol Video Inference Engine，一个基于智能体（agentic）、无需训练的加速框架，通过缓存、稀疏注意力、token剪枝、量化和 kernel 融合五种技术的实例特定组合，在三个不同规模的视频扩散模型上实现超过 2 倍端到端加速，同时保持近乎无损的 VBench 质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频扩散模型通过扩展规模提升生成质量，但推理成本随之剧增。核心挑战在于最优加速策略高度依赖于具体实例——模型架构、硬件平台、推理配置的组合不同，最佳方案差异巨大，导致人工性能工程成本高昂。

Method: 提出 Sol Video Inference Engine，一个 agentic 原生加速框架。其包含五个并行技能代理（cache、sparse attention、token pruning、quantization、kernel fusion），分别优化各自技术实现；一个代理集成器将其组合为全局加速栈；并由人工验证者提供生成质量反馈。整个流程面向具体部署目标（模型+硬件+推理配置）进行实例特定优化。

Result: 在三个不同规模和架构的视频模型上验证——64B Cosmos3-Super、22B LTX-2.3 和 2B SANA-Video，以极少的人工介入实现了超过 2 倍的端到端加速，VBench 质量近乎无损。

Conclusion: 该代理框架在视频扩散推理加速中兼具通用性与实例特异性，证明了 agentic 方法在自动性能工程中的有效性，为大规模视频生成模型的实用部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Modern video diffusion models achieve higher generation quality through scaling, but this also increases inference cost. Although many acceleration methods have been proposed, a central challenge is that the most effective acceleration strategy is highly instance-specific: a recipe that works well for one combination of model, hardware, and inference configuration often does not transfer to another. Different models vary in architecture, numerical sensitivity, and attention concentration patterns. Inference settings differ in spatial and temporal resolution and video duration, while hardware platforms differ in memory hierarchy, supported numerical formats, and kernel throughput. These factors create a large tuning space, making manual performance engineering costly. We present Sol Video Inference Engine, an agentic, native, training-free acceleration framework for video diffusion models. It organizes five broadly applicable techniques, cache, sparse attention, token pruning, quantization, and kernel fusion, into an agentic acceleration stack for instance-specific optimization. For a concrete deployment target defined by a model, hardware platform, and serving configuration, parallel skill agents optimize the implementation of each technique, an agent integrator composes them into a global acceleration stack, and a human validator provides feedback on generation quality. We instantiate this workflow on three video models with different sizes and architectures: 64B Cosmos3-Super, 22B LTX-2.3, and 2B SANA-Video. With little human effort, the full stack achieves more than 2x end-to-end acceleration while maintaining near-lossless VBench quality, demonstrating the effectiveness of the agent framework for video diffusion acceleration.

</details>


### [2] [Autonomous Video Generation with Counterfactual Controllability for Self-Evolving World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24152)
*Xin Wang,Wenxuan Liu,Tongtong Feng,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文质疑"视频生成即世界建模"的流行论断，指出当前视频生成模型仅习得了部分、隐式的时空世界模型，缺乏反事实可控性。作者提出，真正的世界模型需具备反事实可控性——即能够预测干预下的未来、通过具身约束验证、并将行动知识反馈至生成过程。因此，自主视频生成与反事实可控性的结合，是实现自演化世界模型的关键路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献将视频生成等同于世界建模，这一论断虽有推动生成式AI超越静态图像的积极作用，但危险之处在于它隐含一个信念：单纯通过扩大视觉预测规模即可自动催生物理智能体。作者认为这一信念缺乏依据，需对"视频生成即世界模型"这一主张进行更精确的限定与纠偏。

Method: 本文并非提出具体算法，而是采用概念分析与论证的方法。作者首先解构了"视频生成即世界建模"这一命题的局限性，指出当前模型学到的只是部分、隐式、不可控的时空表征。进而，作者提出反事实可控性（counterfactual controllability）作为评判世界模型的关键判据——即模型需能回答"在某一动作下会发生什么"、验证生成未来是否符合具身约束、并将行动知识反馈至未来想象（生成）。最终提出将"自主视频生成+反事实可控性"作为实现自演化世界模型的新视角。

Result: 本文的核心贡献在于重新概念化了视频生成与世界建模的关系，明确指出了四个关键缺陷：视频生成模型不知道哪些变量可控、不知道哪些约束属于特定本体、不知道干预下哪些未来仍然有效。由此，将研究前沿从"预测真实感"重新定位为"反事实可控性"驱动的自演化生成。

Conclusion: 本文的结论是：具备反事实可控性的自主视频生成，是实现自演化世界模型的一条有前景的路径。真正的世界模型不应止步于生成视觉逼真的视频，而必须能够支持干预推理、具身约束验证以及行动知识的闭环反馈，从而在生成中实现自我演化。

Abstract: Existing literature claims that video generation essentially is world modelling. On the one hand, the claim is productive because it pushes generative AI beyond static images and toward temporally extended physical scenes. On the other hand, this claim dangerously relies on the belief that scaling visual prediction alone will automatically yield physical agents. We prefer a more accurate statement: video generation models learn a partial, implicit spatiotemporal world model, but not a fully grounded or controllable one. The reason is as follows: a model may generate a plausible video of a drone crossing a forest or a robot arm manipulating a cup, yet still fail to know which variables are controllable, which constraints belong to a particular body and which futures remain valid under intervention. The frontier in essence is not predictive realism alone, instead it emphasizes a self-evolving generative nature that requires the decisive criterion to be counterfactual controllability: the capability of asking what would happen under an action, to test whether the generated future can survive embodiment constraints and to feed the resulting action knowledge back into future imagination (generation). Therefore, in this paper we present a new perspective, i.e., autonomous video generation with counterfactual controllability is one promising way to realize self-evolving world models.

</details>


### [3] [MotifGen: Spatiotemporal interpolation of misaligned satellite images via multi-source generative modeling, in an application to tropical cyclones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24263)
*Clément Dauvilliers,Claire Monteleoni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个面向多源异构卫星数据的生成式模型，用于对热带气旋微波图像进行不规则时间间隔的时空插值，通过自监督训练和融合红外数据显著提升了重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波卫星影像对监测热带气旋降水和强度至关重要，但其重访周期长，容易错过风暴快速演变阶段。不同微波仪器数据的高度异质性（来源变化、时间间隔不规则、地理不对齐、仪器特性各异）使得插值极具挑战，亟需一种能够处理多源异构数据的插值方法。

Method: 提出了一种可应用于多源地理空间数据的生成式模型。训练采用自监督任务：随机掩蔽某一数据源并要求模型重建该源。模型能够整合来自不同微波仪器和红外传感器的数据，处理不规则时间间隔和地理不对齐问题。最终通过生成集合均值进行预测，并引入红外数据作为辅助信息以进一步提升性能。

Result: 自监督训练相比监督训练在连续分级概率评分（CRPS）上显著降低。融合红外与微波数据比仅使用微波数据效果更优。生成模型的集合均值与确定性模型相当，但其生成的功率谱更接近真实观测值。

Conclusion: 这是首个能够在不规则时间间隔下融合多种微波仪器和红外观测来插值热带气旋微波图像的生成式模型，证明了自监督学习和多模态融合在卫星数据时空插值中的有效性。

Abstract: Microwave satellite imagery plays a crucial role in monitoring tropical cyclone precipitation and intensity worldwide, but suffers from long revisit times, potentially missing rapid storm evolution phases. While this raises the need for an interpolation method, it is made challenging by the high level of heterogeneity of microwave data coming from different instruments. In this work, we introduce the first generative model that can be applied to multiple geospatial sources that change across samples, occur at irregular time intervals, are misaligned geographically, and come from instruments with varying characteristics. We apply this model to the case of spatio-temporal interpolation of tropical cyclone microwave images from other microwave and infrared instruments. We train using a self-supervised task in which a random source is masked and reconstructed, and show that it leads to a significant decrease in Continuous Ranked Probability Score over supervised training. We show a further improvement by combining infrared and microwave data compared to microwave only. Using these improvements, the generative model produces an ensemble mean on par with that of a deterministic model, while generating a power spectrum significantly closer to that of true observations. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first generative model that interpolates microwave images of cyclones by combining multiple microwave instruments and infrared observations at irregular time intervals.

</details>


### [4] [Open-Vocabulary BEV Segmentation with 3D-Aware Geometric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24353)
*Hojun Choi,Seulbin Hwang,Dae Jung Kim,Kisung Kim,Hyunjung Shim,Jinhan Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OVBEVSeg，一个面向开放词汇BEV分割的几何感知框架，通过三阶段渐进式3D几何约束解决2D VLM语义提升到BEV空间时的几何不一致问题，在nuScenes上实现SOTA，对未见类别超越闭集方法15.3 mIoU，且推理速度快2.5倍、内存仅0.22倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前BEV感知方法局限于闭集场景，无法应对真实世界中未知类别的识别需求。将视觉-语言模型引入BEV以实现开放词汇能力，但2D VLM语义向3D BEV空间的提升（lifting）存在固有的几何不一致性问题，这是OVBS的核心挑战。

Method: 提出OVBEVSeg框架，基于高效高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）的反投影，通过三个渐进阶段引入鲁棒的3D几何约束：(1) 通过可靠3D投影进行2D到BEV的伪标签生成，实现开放词汇泛化；(2) 在BEV结构约束下进行2D-BEV联合逐场景优化，确保3D几何一致性；(3) 3D几何蒸馏以实现在线推理效率。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，OVBEVSeg在未见类别上超越闭集方法15.3 mIoU；在无新类别真值标签的情况下，与使用多达40%真值标注训练的自监督和半监督基线方法保持竞争力；推理速度是投影方法的2.5倍，内存消耗仅为0.22倍。

Conclusion: OVBEVSeg有效解决了OVBS中的3D几何不一致问题，实现了开放词汇BEV分割的高精度与实时性，展示了VLM在自动驾驶开放世界感知中的潜力。

Abstract: Bird's-eye view (BEV) perception fuses multi-camera images into a unified top-down representation for autonomous driving. Despite recent progress, state-of-the-art methods remain confined to closed-set scenarios, making them vulnerable to unpredictable real-world environments. In this work, we introduce open-vocabulary BEV segmentation (OVBS), which leverages vision-language models (VLMs) to recognize categories beyond the training set while maintaining precise BEV perception and real-time efficiency. A key challenge in OVBS lies in the 3D geometric inconsistency inherent in the ill-posed lifting of 2D VLM semantics into BEV. To address this, we propose OVBEVSeg, a geometry-aware OVBS framework that enhances efficient Gaussian splatting (GS)-based unprojection by leveraging robust 3D geometric constraints across three progressive stages: (1) 2D-to-BEV pseudo-labeling via reliable 3D projection for OV generalization; (2) joint 2D-BEV per-scene optimization with BEV structural constraints for 3D geometric consistency; and (3) 3D geometric distillation for online efficiency. On the nuScenes dataset, OVBEVSeg achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming closed-set methods by 15.3 mIoU on unseen categories. Remarkably, even with no novel-class ground-truth labels, it remains competitive with self- and semi-supervised baselines trained with up to 40% of ground-truth annotations. Furthermore, it achieves 2.5x faster inference with only 0.22x the memory consumption of projection-based methods. Project page: https://hchoi256.github.io/projects/ovbevseg/.

</details>


### [5] [MedPCFM: Improving Medical Point Cloud Completion by Integrating Point Transformers and Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24433)
*Kamil Kwarciak,Marek Wodzinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PCFM，一种基于PTv3骨干网络与流匹配（Flow Matching）的连续时间生成模型，用于医学点云补全。在三个颅颌面数据集上，PCFM达到最先进的生成性能，且采样步数远少于扩散模型，配合PTv3可获得高达7倍的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学点云补全对解剖重建及下游临床工作流程至关重要，但该领域中的生成式建模研究尚不充分。现有扩散模型虽性能优越，但采样步数多、推理速度慢，亟需一种高效且高质量的生成式补全方法。

Method: 提出PCFM（Point Cloud Flow Matching），采用PTv3作为骨干网络，基于连续时间流匹配框架进行点云生成补全。同时构建了两种强基线：确定性编码器-解码器补全模型（PTv3适配）和扩散补全模型PCDiff（分别使用PVCNN和PTv3作为去噪器）。在SkullFix、SkullBreak和Mandibular Defect三个数据集上评估，并探索了模型规模与点云基数的经验缩放规律。

Result: PCFM+PTv3在各数据集上达到最先进生成性能，与确定性PTv3基线表现相当，但采样步数远少于扩散模型。在最佳运行点，PTv3相比PVCNN为PCFM带来最高7倍加速。缩放实验表明，提高点分辨率带来持续性能增益，不同模型规模间存在信息丰富的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 基于PTv3的流匹配方法PCFM在医学点云补全中实现了生成质量与推理效率的最优平衡，显著优于扩散模型，为该领域提供了高效且可扩展的生成式解决方案。

Abstract: Medical point cloud completion is important for anatomical reconstruction and downstream clinical workflows, yet generative modeling in this setting remains insufficiently studied. We investigate completion through continuous-time generative modeling and introduce PCFM, a PTv3-backed flow matching approach for medical point cloud completion. We evaluate on SkullFix and SkullBreak, and additionally on the more recent Mandibular Defect dataset. We build strong baselines by adapting PTv3 to a deterministic encoder-decoder completion model and by instantiating diffusion completion (PCDiff) with both PVCNN and PTv3 denoisers. PCFM with PTv3 is competitive with the deterministic PTv3 baseline and achieves state-of-the-art generative performance across datasets, while requiring substantially fewer sampling steps than diffusion. At the best operating points, PTv3 also yields clear throughput gains, providing up to a 7$\times$ speed-up for PCFM compared to a PVCNN backbone. Finally, we study empirical scaling trends by varying model size and point cardinality, showing consistent gains with higher point resolution and informative trade-offs across model scales.

</details>


### [6] [EERLoss: A Novel Loss Function for Training Deep Biometric Models. A Case Study in Keystroke Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24586)
*Nahuel Gonzalez,Marta Robledo-Moreno,Ivan DeAndres-Tame,Ruben Vera-Rodriguez,Ruben Tolosana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EERLoss——一种可次微分、任意精度逼近等错误率(EER)的损失函数，用于训练深度生物特征验证模型。在185,000+受试者的大规模击键动力学数据集上，EERLoss相比现有最优损失函数收敛更快，并在重新训练KVC获奖架构后实现约30%的相对EER降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度生物特征验证方法通常优化间接目标（如对比损失、分类损失），与最终评估指标EER之间存在错位。作者旨在设计一个直接对齐EER这一任务评价指标的损失函数，以消除训练与评估之间的目标不一致性。

Method: 提出EERLoss：一种次可微的、可任意精度逼近EER的损失函数。该框架可推广至优化DET曲线上任意特定工作点。通过在击键动力学验证任务上进行实验，包括与现有SOTA损失函数的消融对比，以及使用EERLoss重新训练KVC竞赛获奖架构并与原始SOTA比较。

Result: 消融实验表明EERLoss优于现有SOTA损失函数，且收敛速度显著更快，降低训练成本。将EERLoss应用于KVC获奖架构进行重新训练后，相较原始SOTA实现了最高约30%的相对EER降低。

Conclusion: EERLoss作为一种任务对齐的训练目标，特别适用于高类内变异、低类间变异的生物特征模态（如击键动力学），在大规模基准上验证了其有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Deep learning approaches to biometric verification are commonly trained by optimizing indirect objectives, creating a misalignment between the optimization process and the primary evaluation metric, typically the Equal Error Rate (EER). This paper introduces EERLoss: a subdifferentiable, arbitrarily accurate approximation to EER for training deep biometric models. Furthermore, this framework has the potential to be adapted to optimize any specific operating point on the DET curve, enhancing its generalizability. To validate this approach, EERLoss is evaluated on a particularly demanding behavioral biometric modality: keystroke dynamics verification. This task is characterized by its high intra-class and low inter-class variability. Experiments are conducted on the large-scale KVC-onGoing benchmark, incorporating data from over 185,000 subjects across different scenarios. A comprehensive ablation study initially demonstrates the superiority of EERLoss in comparison to existing state-of-the-art loss functions. It also converges substantially faster compared to other losses, reducing the overall training cost. Additionally, a comparison is made between the proposed loss and the KVC-winning architecture by re-training it with EERLoss, demonstrating that the proposed approach significantly outperforms the original SoTA, achieving a relative EER reduction of up to approx. 30\%. This improvement on a challenging, large-scale benchmark validates the effectiveness of EERLoss as a task-aligned training objective specifically suited for high-variance biometric traits.

</details>

### [7] [Self-Recognition Finetuning can Prevent and Reverse Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23700)
*Arush Tagade,Shaoheng Zhou,Jiaxin Wen,Shi Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 涌现性失对齐(EM)源于模型对齐人格的破坏而非恶意内容的学习。本文提出自生成文本识别(SGTR)微调作为一种面向人格的防御手段,通过两阶段微调实验证明SGTR在逆转和预防EM方面均有效,并揭示EM的本质是对齐人格的失稳而非连贯失对齐人格的采纳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明EM与失对齐人格向量的激活相关,暗示EM通过破坏模型的对齐人格而非直接学习有害内容来发挥作用。基于此,作者希望探索一种面向人格的干预手段(SGTR微调),区别于现有的训练中防御方法,以更好地理解和防御EM。

Method: 作者在三个模型(GPT-4.1、Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct、Seed-OSS-36B-Instruct)和多个EM数据集上进行两阶段微调实验:第一阶段用EM数据诱导失对齐,第二阶段用不同干预手段进行逆转或预防。将SGTR微调与良性微调基线(领域正确数据、通用知识、词计数)对比,并通过身份自报多样性分析、自识别腐化实验、移除身份承载系统提示等方式验证EM与默认人格的关系。

Result: 所有干预在逆转EM方面效果相当,但仅在恢复EM已退化的能力时才有效;预防方面,仅SGTR微调能一致降低失对齐且不恶化任何单项指标;EM微调诱导LLM身份自报多样性增加;人为腐化自识别会加剧EM失对齐;移除模型的身份承载系统提示显著减弱EM微调的效果。

Conclusion: EM应被重新理解为模型对齐人格的失稳(destabilization),而非采纳一个连贯的失对齐人格。SGTR微调通过人格强化实现对EM的有效预防,为防御EM提供了新范式。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM) has been linked to the activation of misaligned persona vectors and evil character traits, suggesting that EM operates through disruption of the model's aligned character rather than direct learning of harmful content. Motivated by this connection, we study self-generated text recognition (SGTR) finetuning as a character-targeted intervention that is distinct from existing in-training defenses. We conduct two-stage finetuning experiments across three models (GPT-4.1, Qwen2.5-32B-Instruct, Seed-OSS-36B-Instruct) and multiple EM datasets to compare SGTR finetuning against benign finetuning baselines (correct domain-specific data, general knowledge, and word counting) to find it an effective defense in both reversal and prevention settings. We find that all interventions produce comparable EM reversal, but only when restoring capabilities that EM had degraded. For prevention, only SGTR finetuning consistently reduces misalignment without exacerbating any individual metric, suggesting that character fortification specifically drives prevention. We provide further evidence for EM's relation to the LLM's default character by showing that EM finetuning induces diversity into the LLM's identity self-reports, artificially corrupting self-recognition exacerbates misalignment caused by EM finetuning, and that removing the model's identity-bearing system prompt substantially reduces the effect of EM finetuning. Together, these findings reframe EM not as the adoption of a coherent misaligned persona but as the destabilization of aligned character.

</details>


### [8] [Evaluating LLM Usage for Efficient and Explainable Numerical and Classified Implicit Sentiment Analysis of Product Desirability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23701)
*Sherri Weitl-Harms,John Hastings*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用大语言模型从定性产品反馈中量化产品满意度的可扩展框架，在零样本设定下，LLM生成的数值情感分数与专家标注高度相关（Pearson r最高0.97，分类准确率最高94%），且GPT-4o-mini以低94%的成本达到与更大模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定性产品反馈蕴含丰富的用户情感信息，但隐式情感难以量化。传统基于词典或Transformer的方法效果不佳，需要一种可扩展、可解释且低成本的方法来自动从定性反馈中提取情感分数。

Method: 使用Product Desirability Toolkit (PDT)数据集（ZORQ和CARMA，共106个受访者术语分组及人工标注），在零样本设定下评估LLM的连续数值情感评分和类别情感分类能力。框架整合了模型置信度评分和可解释AI（xAI）的推理说明，同时对比了基于词典的方法和Transformer基线模型。

Result: LLM直接生成的情感分数与专家标注高度匹配：Pearson相关系数最高达0.97，分类准确率最高达94%。LLM在处理多种数据形式时表现稳健，且置信度一致较高。基于词典和Transformer的基线未产生统计显著结果。GPT-4o-mini以低94%的成本达到与更大模型相当的性能。

Conclusion: 将PDT调查工具与成本高效的LLM相结合进行情感分析，可为产品评估提供丰富的情感分数（数值和分类）以及高层用户印象，有助于识别产品开发改进方向和目标受众营销创意，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Qualitative product feedback can reveal nuanced user experiences, but its implicit sentiment is difficult to measure. This paper presents a scalable and interpretable framework that uses large language models (LLMs) to quantify product desirability from such data. Using two Product Desirability Toolkit (PDT) datasets from ZORQ and CARMA comprising 106 respondent term groupings with gold-standard human annotation, zero-shot continuous numerical sentiment scoring and categorical sentiment classification are evaluated without relying on explicit review scores. Across the datasets, LLMs generated numerical sentiment scores directly from qualitative responses and closely matched expert labels, achieving Pearson correlations up to 0.97 and classification accuracy up to 94%. LLMs maintained robustness even when handling data presented in multiple forms and consistently expressed high confidence. In contrast, lexicon-based and transformer baselines did not produce statistically significant results. Among the models tested, GPT-4o-mini achieved performance comparable to larger models at 94% lower cost, supporting scalable deployment.
  The framework also incorporates model confidence ratings and human-readable rationale explanations (xAI), improving interpretability, transparency, and trust while supporting practical use in product satisfaction assessment. In general, using the PDT tool as a survey method along with a cost efficient LLM for sentiment analysis has the potential to provide for product evaluation with results that are rich in terms of sentiment scores (both numerical and classified sentiment) and in terms of the high-level user impressions of the product that can be used to identify ideas for product development and improvement, as well as marketing ideas for target audiences.

</details>


### [9] [Do LLM Attribution Metrics Transfer? Auditing Retrieval-Augmented Generation Evaluation Across Datasets and Constructs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23915)
*Tianyu Ding,Aditya Nannapaneni,Juan Pablo De la Cruz Weinstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对检索增强生成中归因自动评估指标的审计表明，没有任何一个自动评分器能在所有数据集上保持稳定表现：指标排名在不同数据集间发生反转（Kendall tau = -0.64），在短文本上最优的NLI模型在长文本上降至随机水平（AUROC 0.53），而BERTScore则相反。基于LLM的评判器避免了崩溃但成本高且非确定性，验证负担并未消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中常将LLM检索增强生成的归因自动评估指标视为可互换使用，但缺乏对其跨数据集、跨评估构念一致性的系统审计。作者旨在揭示这种"互换性"假设是否成立，以及指标选择不当带来的实际决策成本。

Method: 审计八种自动评分器（词汇基线、嵌入基线、BERTScore基线，以及基于蕴含/接地训练的模型如NLI、MiniCheck等），在三个评估构念（来源/主题性、生成答案归因、事实验证蕴含）上测试。核心方法：检验是否有评分器能在每个多数据集构念的所有数据集上保持在最佳评分器95%置信区间内（即可迁移性）。在生成答案归因构念上使用AttributionBench四个源数据集（n=1,610）和独立HAGRID（n=2,150），计算Kendall tau排名相关性，并进行留一数据集遗憾值分析。

Result: 无任何评分器可迁移：AttributionBench上指标排名在数据集间显著反转（Kendall tau = -0.64, p = 0.031，AttributedQA vs. LFQA）。现成NLI评分器在短文本AttributedQA上最优（AUROC 0.90），但在长文本LFQA上降至随机水平（AUROC 0.53），而BERTScore在该场景胜出（0.91），且非长度或截断伪影。基于平均最优的选择策略在留一数据集评估中失败（平均保留遗憾0.172 AUROC）。LLM评判器避免了自动评分器的崩溃，但非统一最优、成本约高100倍且非确定性。

Conclusion: 自动归因评估指标不可互换使用，指标选择必须在目标数据集上验证，不能从其他数据集推断。LLM评判器虽更稳健，但只是转移而非消除了验证负担。研究呼吁开发更鲁棒的归因评估方法。

Abstract: Practice often treats automatic metrics for attribution in LLM retrieval-augmented generation as interchangeable. We audit eight automatic scorers -- lexical, embedding, and BERTScore baselines alongside entailment/grounding-trained models (clean and FEVER NLI, the checker MiniCheck) -- across three evaluation constructs (provenance/topicality, generated-answer attribution, and fact-check entailment), asking whether any scorer transfers: stays within the 95% confidence interval of the best audited scorer on every dataset of a multi-dataset construct. In the construct with the most multi-dataset human-labeled coverage -- generated-answer attribution (AttributionBench's four source datasets, n = 1,610, with independent HAGRID, n = 2,150) -- none does: the per-dataset metric rankings invert (Kendall tau = -0.64, p = 0.031 on AttributedQA vs. LFQA), and an off-the-shelf NLI scorer that is best on short-claim AttributedQA (AUROC 0.90) collapses to AUROC 0.53 (chance) on long-form LFQA, where BERTScore wins (0.91); the flip is not a length or truncation artifact. This instability has a concrete decision cost: a naive "best-on-average" rule for choosing an evaluator fails leave-one-dataset-out (mean held-out regret 0.172 AUROC, worse than fixing one scorer), so metric choice must be validated on the target dataset rather than learned from others. A prompt-based LLM judge avoids the chance-level collapses the automatic scorers suffer (no LFQA collapse) but is not uniformly best, ~100x costlier, and non-deterministic -- relocating, not removing, the validation burden.

</details>


### [10] [When Retrieval Metrics Mislead: Measuring Policy Signal in Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23937)
*Tianyu Ding,Juan Pablo De la Cruz Weinstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在tau-bench环境中测试了精确匹配检索召回率作为下游决策模型策略上下文效用代理指标的可靠性。实验表明，即使精确策略条款的检索召回率很低（仅7%），下游分类器的宏观F1值仅从0.60降至0.58，差异不显著，说明精确匹配召回率可能低估了检索策略的实际下游效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确匹配检索召回率常被用作衡量检索器是否为下游决策模型提供有用策略上下文的代理指标，但这一代理指标的可靠性尚未经过充分验证。作者旨在检验在预行动策略分类任务中，该代理指标是否能真实反映检索结果对下游决策模型的实用价值。

Method: 使用tau-bench环境，以Qwen2.5-3B/7B作为分类器进行预行动策略分类。首先在gold-policy条件下，对比紧凑结构化状态与原始轨迹两种输入格式的分类性能。随后将基准测试指定的策略条款替换为检索器在决策时刻返回的排名第一的条款，比较使用检索条款与使用gold条款时的宏观F1值，并设置策略不匹配和无策略两个对照组。

Result: 紧凑结构化状态相比原始轨迹使宏观F1提升0.13-0.17。航空领域状态中仅7%的查询能将精确条款排在首位，但3B分类器使用检索条款的宏观F1为0.58，与使用gold条款的0.60相比仅下降0.02（95%置信区间[-0.23, +0.21]），远优于策略不匹配（0.32）和无策略（0.21）对照组。7B模型和第二检索器均呈现相同模式，但结果因微调配置而异。

Conclusion: 精确匹配条款召回率在此基准设定中可能低估了检索策略的下游效用，因此建议在评估检索器时，应将检索到的策略纳入分类循环中进行端到端评估，而非仅依赖召回率指标。

Abstract: Exact-match retrieval recall is often used as a proxy for whether a retriever supplies useful policy context to a downstream decision model. We test this proxy for pre-action policy classification in tau-bench using Qwen2.5-3B/7B classifiers. Under gold-policy conditioning, a compact structured state improves macro-F1 over raw trajectories by 0.13-0.17 after tuning. We then replace the benchmark-designated policy clause with the top-ranked clause retrieved from decision-time context. Although the exact governing clause is retrieved at rank 1 for only 7% of airline states, the primary 3B classifier obtains macro-F1 0.58 with retrieved clauses versus 0.60 with gold clauses (Delta=-0.02, task-cluster 95% CI [-0.23,+0.21]); mismatched-policy and no-policy controls score 0.32 and 0.21. We do not detect a macro-F1 difference between retrieved and gold clauses in this configuration, although the interval remains too wide to establish non-inferiority. The same qualitative pattern appears with a second retriever and at 7B, while varying across fine-tuning configurations. These results indicate that exact-match clause recall can underestimate downstream policy utility in this benchmark setting, motivating evaluation with retrieved policies in the classification loop rather than recall alone.

</details>


### [11] [Does My Embedding Reflect That $A = B$? Evaluating Mathematical Equivalence in Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23959)
*Jiaying Ye,Samarth Rao,Leo Carlin,Kedar Chintalapati,Saharsh Bhargava,Rachit Jaiswal,Michael Zhou,Jared Darlington,Jarod Alper,Vasily Ilin,Henry Kvinge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了当前嵌入模型是否能够捕捉数学等价性，发现现有模型倾向于按术语而非底层数学语义分组。为此，作者提出了一种对比学习方法，通过对齐非正式陈述与不同形式化表述来改进数学文本的嵌入表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学具有高度抽象性，同一数学命题在不同子领域中可能以截然不同的语言形式呈现。历史上许多突破源于研究者发现某个问题已在另一领域被解决。同时，形式化相关资源的增长催生了对跨数学"语言"（如Lean与自然语言之间）高效可靠导航工具的需求。然而，当前嵌入模型是否真正捕捉到了数学等价性尚不明确。

Method: （1）构建MELD（Mathematically Equivalent but Lexically Different Pairs）数据集，收集语义等价但语言表达差异显著的数学陈述对；（2）提出一种对比学习方法，专注于对齐非正式数学陈述与其不同的形式化表述，以此训练嵌入模型更好地捕捉底层数学等价性。

Result: 当前最先进的嵌入模型倾向于根据术语相似性而非底层数学语义对陈述进行分组；所提出的对比学习方法不仅在非正式-形式化检索任务上取得提升，在仅包含自然语言陈述的MELD数据集上也表现出改进。

Conclusion: 现有嵌入模型对数学等价性的捕捉能力不足，而通过对比学习对齐非正式与形式化表述可以有效改善这一问题，为跨数学语言的语义检索提供了新思路。

Abstract: Because mathematics is highly abstract, a single statement can take very different forms depending on what subfield it is framed in. There are many examples where breakthroughs occurred after researchers discovered that a question had already been answered in a different field. At the same time, the growth of new resources related to formalization has increased the need for tools that enable efficient and reliable navigation between mathematical 'languages' (e.g., from Lean to natural language). In this paper, we investigate whether current embedding models capture mathematical equivalence. To do this, we introduce the Mathematically Equivalent but Lexically Different Pairs (MELD) Dataset, a collection of mathematically equivalent statements that are expressed in very different language. We show that current state-of-the-art embedding models tend to group statements by the terminology used to make them instead of the underlying math. Motivated by this, we propose a contrastive approach to learning embeddings of mathematical text that focuses on aligning informal statements with different formalizations. Our experiments demonstrate that this leads to improvements not only on informal-formal retrieval tasks but also on MELD, which only contains natural language statements.

</details>


### [12] [RASC+: Retrieval-Constrained LLM Adjudication for Clinical Value Set Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23992)
*Sumit Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究临床价值集自动补全任务，提出一种两阶段方法：第一阶段通过词汇感知扩展和代码展示救援检索优化候选池召回率（从0.553提升至0.730），第二阶段使用受限LLM（盲化GPT-5）进行候选筛选，最终在全测试集上macro F1达到0.549，在留出出版社分层上达到0.533，显著优于直接零样本生成和SAPBert交叉编码器方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床价值集补全（Value Set Completion）是质量测量、表型分析和临床决策支持的关键任务。现有RASC基准表明，直接使用LLM零样本生成效果不佳，因为临床代码系统规模庞大、版本受控且无法被语言模型可靠记忆。因此需要一种既保证召回又确保所有返回代码可审计的安全方法。

Method: 提出两阶段架构：阶段一（候选池构建）采用基于Qwen3的检索，结合词汇感知扩展（vocabulary-aware expansion）和代码展示救援检索（code-display rescue retrieval）最大化候选召回率；阶段二（候选选择）将扩展后的候选池交由盲化GPT-5进行受限裁决（constrained LLM adjudication），确保所有返回代码均来自可审计的候选池。

Result: 在3,744个价值集的RASC测试集上，候选池召回率从基线0.553提升至0.730（留出出版社分层为0.655）。使用SAPBert交叉编码器在扩展池上仅得macro F1 0.287（留出出版社0.233），而替换为盲化GPT-5后macro F1大幅提升至0.549（留出出版社0.533）。

Conclusion: 检索约束的LLM裁决可在保证安全约束（所有返回代码源自可审计候选池）的前提下显著提升价值集补全性能，验证了"检索扩召回 + LLM精选"两阶段范式的有效性。

Abstract: Clinical value sets define the standardized terminology codes used in quality measurement, phenotyping, cohort construction, and clinical decision support. The recently introduced Retrieval-Augmented Set Completion (RASC) benchmark showed that direct zero-shot large language model (LLM) generation is poorly suited to this task: clinical code systems are large, version-controlled, and not reliably memorized by language models. We study a stage-wise alternative in which candidate-pool construction is optimized for recall and a constrained LLM adjudicator is optimized for candidate selection. On the full 3,744-value-set RASC test split, Qwen3-based retrieval with vocabulary-aware expansion and code-display rescue retrieval increases candidate-pool recall from the original RASC retrieval baseline of 0.553 to 0.730; on the held-out-publisher stratum, pool recall is 0.655. The higher-recall pool alone is not sufficient: applying the original SAPBert cross-encoder to this expanded pool gives full-test macro F1 of 0.287 and held-out-publisher macro F1 of 0.233. Replacing the stage-2 selector with blinded GPT-5 adjudication over the same pool increases full-test macro F1 to 0.549 and held-out-publisher macro F1 to 0.533. These results show that retrieval-constrained LLM adjudication can substantially improve value set completion while preserving the safety constraint that all returned codes must come from an auditable candidate pool.

</details>


### [13] [CAVEWOMAN: How Large Language Models Behave Under Linguistic Input and Output Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24083)
*Morayo Danielle Adeyemi,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Cavewoman双通道评估协议，系统研究提示压缩与输出压缩对推理成本、任务精度及文本一致性的影响。核心发现：输出压缩可节省1.4-2.4倍成本，而输入压缩反而增加成本（约1.15倍），因为模型会以更长回复补偿，同时精度崩塌；且约半数正确生成的表面文本不再蕴含基线生成内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "精简说话、省略语法、节省token"的"穴居人风格"被广泛宣传为降低推理成本的方法，但缺乏系统性评估来区分压缩发生在哪个通道（用户提示 vs. 模型回复）及其实际效果。作者旨在揭示不同压缩通道对成本、精度和语义保真度的差异化影响。

Method: 提出Cavewoman双通道评估协议，在五个数据集上对八个模型（含API模型和开放权重模型）、五个压缩级别进行评测。每个生成在三个维度上打分：任务精度（task accuracy）、实际每项成本（realized per-item cost）、以及与模型自身无约束基线生成的参考文本一致性（reference-text agreement）。两通道在同一项目上同步测量。

Result: 输出压缩在多数API模型上实现1.4-2.4倍成本削减（最佳达3倍），在公开定价的开放权重模型上均有效。输入压缩效果相反：净成本不降反升（五基准均值约1.15倍，最差数据集达1.8倍，强压缩下达2.7倍），因模型以更长回复补偿，且精度崩溃。在非推理模型上，约半数正确生成的表面文本不再蕴含模型自身无约束基线的生成内容，该分歧经长度控制重评分、多重比较校正及补充语义度量验证后依然稳健。

Conclusion: 压缩效果高度依赖压缩通道：输出压缩是有效的降本策略，而输入压缩是严格的双输方案（成本上升且精度下降）。此外，即使生成结果正确，其表面文本也常偏离模型无约束时的自然表达，提示压缩会实质改变模型行为模式。

Abstract: "Talk short. Drop grammar. Save token." This caveman style is widely promoted as a way to cut inference cost, but whether it actually saves anything depends on which channel (the user's prompt or the model's response) is being compressed. We present Cavewoman, a two-channel evaluation protocol that scores every generation on task accuracy, realized per-item cost, and reference-text agreement against the model's unconstrained reference. We evaluate eight models on five datasets at five reduction levels, with both channels measured on the same items. Output compression cuts realized cost on most API models (1.4-2.4x per model, up to 3x in the best case) and on all four open-weight models under public-tier pricing. Input compression has the opposite effect, a strict lose-lose: it raises net cost rather than lowering it (~1.15x on the five-benchmark mean, up to 1.8x on the worst dataset and 2.7x under stronger compression), because models compensate with longer responses even as accuracy collapses. Under the same setting, surface text diverges from the unconstrained reference: on the non-reasoning models, roughly half of all generations are correct yet their surface text no longer entails the model's own unconstrained baseline generation. The divergence survives length-controlled re-scoring, multiple-comparisons correction, and replication under complementary semantic measures. Code and data are available at https://github.com/danielle34/cavewoman.

</details>


### [14] [PORTER: Language-Grounded Event Representations for Portable Structured EHR Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24102)
*Lin Lawrence Guo,Adam Paul Yan,Emily Vettese,Lillian Sung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PORTER 是一种语言基础的结构化 EHR 基础模型，通过冻结的文本编码器描述事件并集成数值通道，实现了跨词汇表的迁移能力，在74项临床预测任务中匹配固定词汇模型性能，且无需重新训练即可迁移至未见过的词汇表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 EHR 基础模型将临床事件编码为固定词汇表中的离散 token，无法表示未见过的新概念或新的概念-属性组合（如数值），限制了跨机构和跨部署管线的迁移能力。

Method: 提出 PORTER 模型：(1) 使用冻结的文本编码器通过事件描述来表示事件，解耦事件表示与固定词汇表；(2) 通过专用通道集成数值；(3) 使用自回归预训练的时间骨干网络学习患者时间线上的临床动态。

Result: 在儿科医院的74项临床预测任务中，PORTER 的 AUROC 均值与固定词汇模型持平；使用未见过的事件描述时，无需重新训练即可恢复目标词汇直接训练模型 97.1% 的 AUROC；迁移至 MIMIC 时，PORTER 优于固定词汇模型（后者丢失 69% 的事件）；数值通道提升了对量值的敏感性且不破坏临床概念表征；计算开销仅为任务特定文本序列化对比方法的 1/329。

Conclusion: PORTER 是迈向词汇表无关 EHR 基础模型的一步，减少了词汇表协调需求，同时保持领域内性能并支持高效的跨任务复用。

Abstract: Most electronic health record (EHR) foundation models encode clinical events as discrete event tokens from a fixed vocabulary and therefore cannot directly represent events containing unseen concepts or new combinations of concepts and attributes such as numeric values. This limits transfer across institutions and even across deployment pipelines within the same institution. We introduce PORTER, a language-grounded structured EHR foundation model that decouples event representation from this fixed vocabulary. PORTER represents events through their descriptions using a frozen text encoder, integrates numeric values through a dedicated pathway, and learns clinical dynamics over patient timelines with an autoregressively pretrained temporal backbone. Across 74 clinical prediction tasks at a pediatric hospital, PORTER matched the mean AUROC of a fixed-vocabulary model with the same temporal backbone and pretraining objective. When the same patient timelines were rendered using event descriptions not seen during pretraining, PORTER transferred without retraining or vocabulary mapping, recovering 97.1% of the mean AUROC of a model trained directly on the target vocabulary. When transferred to MIMIC, PORTER outperformed the fixed-vocabulary model, which dropped 69% of events because their tokens were unseen. Mechanistic analyses showed cross-vocabulary transfer tracked preservation of patient-level representation geometry rather than the scale of the text encoder, and the numeric pathway improved sensitivity to magnitude without disrupting clinical concept identity. PORTER also achieved higher AUROC than a task-specific text serialization comparator, at 329-fold lower amortized compute. PORTER is a step toward vocabulary-independent EHR foundation models that reduce the need for vocabulary harmonization while preserving in-domain performance and enabling efficient cross-task reuse.

</details>


### [15] [BehaviorBench: Benchmarking Foundation Models for Behavioral Science Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24162)
*Jin Huang,Yutong Xie,Wanli Song,Xingjian Zhang,Walter Yuan,Matthew O. Jackson,Qiaozhu Mei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 BehaviorBench，一个面向行为科学的综合基准测试，从行为预测与模拟、策略决策、主体特质推断和行为知识应用四个维度评估基础模型，强调个体与分布双重层面的评估。同时开发了 Be.FM-1.5 行为基础模型，结果表明专有通用模型在个体预测上占优，而行为微调模型在分布对齐上更强，凸显了分布评估的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在心理学、社会学、经济学等行为科学领域的应用日益增多，但缺乏对它们跨任务、跨情境、跨人群表现的系统性理解。现有评估多以个体任务为主，忽视群体分布层面的行为有效性验证。

Method: 构建 BehaviorBench 基准，涵盖四大核心能力维度：(1) 行为预测与模拟，(2) 策略决策，(3) 主体特质推断，(4) 行为知识应用。评估采用个体级和分布级双重指标。基于该基准进一步微调开发 Be.FM-1.5 模型，扩展 Be.FM 行为基础模型家族。

Result: 专有通用模型在个体级预测和知识密集型任务上表现优异；经行为数据微调的 Be.FM-1.5 在分布对齐指标上显著领先，同时在个体级指标上保持竞争力，表明适当的行为适配可弥合差距。

Conclusion: 分布级评估对行为科学 AI 系统至关重要。BehaviorBench 可作为开发和评估行为对齐 AI 系统的基础平台，Be.FM-1.5 在广泛的行为科学研究中展现出良好应用潜力。

Abstract: Foundation models have been increasingly applied to behavioral science domains such as psychology, sociology, and economics. While these models show promise in individual tasks such as survey response prediction and human-subject experiment simulation, there remains no systematic understanding of how well they perform across diverse behavioral science tasks, contexts, and populations. We introduce BehaviorBench, a comprehensive benchmark that evaluates foundation models along four core capabilities: (1) behavior prediction and simulation, (2) strategic decision-making, (3) subject-trait inference, and (4) behavioral knowledge application. Crucially, BehaviorBench evaluates model outputs at both the individual and distributional levels, capturing not only per-subject accuracy but also population-level alignment, an essential requirement for behavioral validity. Leveraging the tasks in BehaviorBench, we further develop Be.FM-1.5, extending the Be.FM family of behavioral foundation models fine-tuned on behavioral data. Our results reveal a considerable gap: proprietary general-purpose models excel at individual-level prediction and knowledge-intensive tasks, whereas behavioral foundation models, fine-tuned on behavioral data, achieve substantially stronger distributional alignment. Notably, Be.FM-1.5 leads on distributional metrics and remains competitive on individual-level metrics, suggesting that proper behavioral adaptation can close the gap. Our results highlight the importance of distributional evaluation, establish BehaviorBench as a foundation for developing and assessing behaviorally aligned AI systems, and demonstrate Be.FM-1.5's potential for a broad range of behavioral science studies. Our BehaviorBench and Be.FM-1.5 models can be accessed via https://umich-foreseer.github.io/behaviorbench/.

</details>


### [16] [A Pāninian Foundation for Indic Language Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24172)
*Ritwik Banerjee,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 印地语系语言共享一套源于梵语和波你尼语法（Astādhyāyī）的形态句法架构，本文主张以这一波你尼框架作为统一的计算基础，提出一个四部分基准套件，以使印地语系NLP更加准确、数据高效和可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超过十亿人使用印地语系语言，但其NLP基础设施分散且不发达。根本原因在于，该领域按单一语言或小语系分别构建分析器、解析器和数据集，忽视了各语言间因两千年梵语趋同而形成的深层共性——即波你尼形态句法架构的统一性。

Method: 作者主张将波你尼语法框架作为印地语系语言的统一计算架构，并设计了一个包含四部分的基准套件（benchmark suite），将该共享架构显式化、可量化，以便用于实际应用。此外，该方法还提出了一个可解释性问题：在此类语言上训练的神经模型是否能自发习得波你尼语法范畴。

Result: 本文为立场性／提案性论文，未报告具体实验结果；其核心成果是提出了该基准套件的设计方案及其理论上可带来的收益——更高的准确性、更高的数据效率、更强的跨语言迁移能力，以及将分散的稀疏资源合并为一个高资源元语言基底。

Conclusion: 波你尼语法框架为印地语系NLP提供了期待已久的统一计算架构。以该架构为基石的基准套件能够弥合语言资源碎片化的问题，并为未来的可解释性研究（探究神经模型是否自发习得波你尼范畴）奠定基础。

Abstract: More than a billion people communicate in Indic languages, yet the natural language processing infrastructure serving them remains fragmented and underdeveloped. The cause is structural: the field organizes its tools and benchmarks around individual languages or small subsets of genealogical language families, building separate analyzers, parsers, and datasets for each language and starting over for the next. This overlooks a deep regularity. Through more than two millennia of convergence around Sanskrit, Indic languages came to share a morphosyntactic architecture formalized in Pānini's grammar, the Astādhyāyī. This cuts across genealogical lines, uniting languages through a common framework. We argue that this Pāninian framework supplies a unifying computational architecture the field has lacked, and that benchmarks grounded explicitly in it would make Indic language systems more accurate, more data-efficient, and more transferable, effectively merging many apparently disparate and sparse Indic language resources into a single high-resource metalanguage bedrock. We propose a four-part benchmark suite to render this shared architecture explicit, measurable, and ready to be leveraged for practical applications. Moreover, we underscore the question it raises for interpretability research: whether neural models trained on these languages come to represent Pānini's categories on their own.

</details>


### [17] [Harmonic: Hierarchical State Space Models for Efficient Long-Context Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24650)
*Petr Nyoma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Harmonic 是一个用于语言建模的分层状态空间模型，通过三个不同时间尺度的循环层级堆叠，每层接收下层的预测误差而非原始隐藏状态，在长序列建模上显著优于同等规模的 Transformer 和 Mamba，同时计算复杂度为 O(L)，并能在 1B 参数规模下消除 RoPE 位置编码的长度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 的注意力机制计算复杂度为 O(L²)，在长序列上内存消耗巨大且受限于位置编码长度；现有 SSM（如 Mamba）虽有线性复杂度但仍有限制。本文旨在设计一种既能高效处理超长序列、又能消除位置编码限制的语言模型架构。

Method: 提出 Harmonic 分层状态空间模型：1）三层循环层级以逐步减慢的时间尺度堆叠；2）每层接收下层输出的预测误差（而非原始隐藏状态）作为输入——即上层对下层难以预测的残差信号进行建模；3）替代 TinyLlama 1.1B 中所有注意力层为 HarmonicBlock，得到 Hallamonic 模型，无需 RoPE 位置编码。

Result: 在 enwiki8 上，同等参数量下 Harmonic 在 1K/8K/32K 长度分别优于 Transformer 1.4%/6.7%/11.4%，优于 Mamba 0.7-1.8%；64K 长度下 Transformer 和 Mamba 均内存溢出，Harmonic 成功训练至 6.169 bpt。Wikitext-103 结果复现（1K-32K 差距 1.7%-7.2%）。在 1B 规模，Hallamonic 在 1K-8K 长度保持稳定 loss，而 TinyLlama 超过其 2K RoPE 限制后性能灾难性下降（8K 时差距 +9.4 bpt）。

Conclusion: Harmonic 通过分层预测误差驱动的 SSM 架构，在保持 O(L) 线性计算复杂度的同时，实现了对超长序列的有效建模，并从根本上消除了注意力机制中位置编码的长度上限，在多个基准和尺度上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: We present Harmonic, a hierarchical state space model (SSM) for language modeling. The architecture stacks three recurrent levels at progressively slower timescales; each level receives the prediction error of the level below as input, rather than its raw hidden state. On enwiki8 with equal token budgets, Harmonic outperforms a comparable Transformer (28M params) by +1.4% at 1K tokens, +6.7% at 8K tokens, and +11.4% at 32K tokens (bpt, lower is better). It also outperforms Mamba at every tested length by 0.7--1.8%. At 64K tokens, both Mamba and Transformer run out of memory on an 80GB H100; Harmonic trains successfully, reaching 6.169 bpt. Results replicate on WikiText-103 (H-TF gap +1.7% to +7.2% across 1K--32K). At 1B parameter scale, replacing all attention layers in TinyLlama 1.1B with HarmonicBlock eliminates the RoPE positional encoding limit: the resulting Hallamonic model maintains stable loss across sequence lengths 1K--8K on two independent clean benchmarks (Lambada and fineweb-edu held-out), while TinyLlama degrades catastrophically past its 2K-token RoPE limit (gap: +9.4 bpt at seq=8K on Lambada). Compute is O(L) per forward pass vs. O(L^2) for attention.
  Logs: https://github.com/Omibranch/harmonic-logs.

</details>


### [18] [AI-PAVE-Br: Leveraging Large Language Models for Enhanced Product Attribute Value Extraction through a Golden Set Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24655)
*Murilo Gazzola,Hugo Gobato Souto,Samuel Silva,Júlia Schubert Peixoto,Felipe Siqueira,André Luis Pedroso de Morais,Caio Gomes*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对巴西电商中葡萄牙语产品属性值抽取（PAVE）的挑战，本文推出基于大语言模型的AI-PAVEBr系统及人工标注的Golden Set基准数据集，实验表明其通过提示工程显著超越传统NER方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西电商产品数据爆炸式增长且高度复杂，传统PAVE方法难以应对葡萄牙语的语言细微差异和产品描述的多样性，因此亟需专为巴西市场定制的结构化信息抽取方案。

Method: 提出AI-PAVEBr系统，基于大语言模型（LLMs），采用针对性提示工程（prompt engineering）实现高精度的产品属性值抽取；同时构建并公开人工精细标注的Golden Set数据集（含实体、类别、子类别结构），用于模型评估与复现研究。

Result: 实验表明，AI-PAVEBr在葡萄牙语PAVE任务上大幅超越传统命名实体识别（NER）基线方法，验证了LLM加提示工程方案在该场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅为巴西这一重要非英语电商市场提供了可扩展的优质PAVE解决方案，还通过公开发布Golden Set数据集，为NLP社区未来研究贡献了宝贵资源。

Abstract: The explosive growth and complexity of product data within the dynamic Brazilian e-commerce landscape demand robust and specialized methods for structured information extraction. Traditional approaches to Product Attribute Value Extraction (PAVE) often struggle with the linguistic nuances and sheer diversity of product descriptions in Portuguese. To address this critical gap, this paper introduces two major contributions. First, we present AI-PAVEBr, a specialized system engineered with Large Language Models (LLMs) to perform high-accuracy PAVE specifically for Brazilian e-commerce catalogs. Second, to facilitate reproducible research and provide a definitive benchmark, we introduce and share the Golden Set, a new, meticulously curated, and manually annotated dataset for PAVE in Portuguese. We detail the creation process and structure (Entity, Category, Subcategories) of this high-quality reference set. Our experiments conclusively show that AI-PAVE-Br, leveraging targeted prompt engineering, dramatically outperforms conventional Named Entity Recognition (NER) baselines. This work not only delivers a superior, scalable solution for a major non-English market but also enriches the NLP community with a valuable, publicly available resource for future PAVE research.

</details>


### [19] [L3Cube-MahaPOS: A Marathi Part-of-Speech Tagging Dataset and BERT Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24825)
*Hariom Ingle,Ronit Ghode,Ishwari Gondkar,Jidnyasa Harad,Raviraj Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: L3Cube-MahaPOS是一个人工标注的马拉地语词性标注数据集，包含32,354句新闻文本，采用16标签UD对齐方案。在六类模型上进行了基准测试，最佳系统达到88.67%的token级准确率和81.67%的宏平均F1值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马拉地语虽有超过8300万使用人口，但在标注语料和标准化评测基准方面严重资源不足。其丰富的形态变化、相对自由的词序、缺乏大小写规范以及与印地语和英语的广泛语码混合，给计算建模带来了独特挑战，亟需高质量标注数据集来推动该语言的NLP研究。

Method: 研究团队构建了L3Cube-MahaPOS数据集，从新闻文本中选取32,354个句子，由熟练掌握马拉地语的标注人员完全手工标注，遵循16标签的通用依存关系对齐方案。预处理流程包括Unicode规范化、天城体感知的分词及噪声过滤，确保各数据划分的标签一致性。在HMM、CRF、BiLSTM、BiLSTM+CharCNN、MuRIL以及马拉地语专用Transformer模型MahaBERT-v2共六种模型族上进行了系统基准评测。

Result: 在15个评估标签类别上，最佳系统达到88.67%的token级准确率和81.67%的宏平均F1值。不同模型族的对比结果揭示了从传统统计方法到专用预训练语言模型在低资源语言词性标注任务上的性能差异。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了马拉地语词性标注领域的高质量基准数据集空白。数据集、标注指南及训练好的模型检查点均已开源，为推动马拉地语NLP后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Part-of-Speech (POS) tagging is a foundational NLP task underpinning machine translation, information extraction, and syntactic parsing. Despite Marathi being spoken by over 83 million people and ranking among the top twenty most spoken languages worldwide, it remains severely under-resourced in annotated corpora and standardised evaluation benchmarks. Marathi presents unique challenges for computational modelling owing to its rich morphology, relatively free word order, lack of capitalisation conventions, and pervasive code-mixing with Hindi and English. We introduce L3Cube-MahaPOS, a gold-standard POS tagging dataset for Marathi comprising 32,354 manually annotated sentences drawn from news text. Annotation was performed entirely manually by a team of Marathi-proficient annotators following a 16-tag Universal Dependencies-aligned scheme. A structured preprocessing pipeline covering Unicode normalisation, Devanagari-aware tokenisation, and noise filtering ensures label consistency across all splits. We benchmark the dataset across six model families spanning HMM, CRF, BiLSTM, BiLSTM+CharCNN, MuRIL, and the Marathi-specific transformer MahaBERT-v2. The best system achieves 88.67\% token-level accuracy and a macro-F1 of 81.67% over 15 evaluated tag classes. We release the dataset, annotation guidelines, and trained model checkpoints to foster further research in Marathi NLP.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

### [20] [Minimax PAC Bounds for Learning in Exogenous Contextual MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25170)
*Corentin Pla,Hugo Richard,Marc Abeille,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究了具有外生i.i.d.上下文的表格折扣MDP的PAC学习问题。根据对上下文分布和转移核的已知程度，分别给出了策略评估、最优值估计和最优策略提取的样本复杂度上下界，在多种设定下达到了极小极大最优，且样本复杂度与上下文空间大小无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习中，上下文（外生变量）常影响奖励和转移但不被智能体控制。现有工作对此类Contextual MDP的样本复杂度刻画不够精细，尤其是对上下文分布的采样代价与转移核的采样代价应如何分配、以及样本复杂度是否依赖于上下文空间大小等问题尚未充分解决。本文旨在系统性地给出不同已知程度下的紧样本复杂度界。

Method: 考虑表格折扣MDP，上下文来自未知分布μ。根据对μ和转移核P的已知程度划分三种设定：(1) P已知、仅μ未知；(2) μ已知、仅P未知；(3) 两者均未知。通过方差缩减技术（variance-reduced algorithm）和构造信息论下界，分别给出策略评估(PE)、最优值估计(BVE)和最优策略提取(BPE)的样本复杂度上下界，使用执行前采样次数n和执行中采样次数m作为联合复杂度度量。

Result: 当P已知仅μ未知时，PE/BVE/BPE的样本复杂度为(Õ(1/((1-γ)³ε²)), 0)，与|Z|无关且极小极大最优；作为推论，在一步完美前瞻情形下也得到紧界。当μ和P均未知时，PE的样本复杂度为(Õ(|X|/((1-γ)³ε²)), Õ(1/((1-γ)²ε²)))，同样与|Z|无关，且匹配上下界。

Conclusion: 在具有外生上下文的表格MDP中，策略评估的样本复杂度可以做到与上下文空间大小|Z|无关，这在多种已知程度设定下均成立。本文给出的上下界在多个情形下匹配，为Contextual MDP的PAC学习提供了较为完整的理论刻画。

Abstract: We study PAC learning in tabular discounted Markov decision processes with exogenous i.i.d. contexts, with discount factor $γ$, finite state space $\mathcal X$, action space $\mathcal A$, and context space $\mathcal Z$. At each time step, a context is drawn independently from an unknown distribution $μ$ and revealed before the agent acts. This context may affect both rewards and transitions, while remaining uncontrolled by the agent. Depending on the regime, the learner has access either to a sampling oracle for $μ$, to a sampling oracle for the transition kernel conditioned on state-context-action tuples, or to both. Oracles can be accessed before and during policy execution. The sample complexity is measured by a couple $(n,m)$, where $n$ is the number of calls to the sampling oracles before execution and $m$ is the number of calls to the sampling oracles during execution. When rewards and transitions are known and only the context distribution $μ$ is sampled, we give a variance-reduced algorithm that solves policy evaluation (PE), best-value estimation (BVE), and best-policy extraction (BPE) with $\left(\widetilde O\left(1/((1-γ)^3\varepsilon^2)\right), 0 \right) $ sample complexity. The rate is independent of $|\mathcal Z|$ and minimax optimal up to logarithmic factors. As a corollary, we also obtain tight rates in the case of one-step perfect look-ahead, improving upon the existing guarantees. In the fully unknown regime, where both $μ$ and P must be learned, we show that PE remains $|\mathcal Z|$-free, with matching upper and lower bounds $\bigl(\widetilde O(|\mathcal X|/((1-γ)^3\varepsilon^2)),\, \widetilde O(1/((1-γ)^2\varepsilon^2))\bigr)$.

</details>


### [21] [Stabilizing black-box algorithms through task-oriented randomization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25269)
*Yali Wang,Zhaojun Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种任务导向的随机化方法，根据输入数据的生成机制自适应调整策略，以稳定黑盒模型输出，并在理论上建立了稳定性保障与探索之间的权衡关系，最终扩展至大语言模型中的top-k排序问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒模型在现代研究中日益普及，但其输入数据的多样性（从结构化高斯分布到未知复杂结构）给输出稳定性带来巨大挑战。如何在利用先验信息的同时有效稳定黑盒输出，是尚未充分解决的问题。

Method: 提出任务导向的随机化方法（task-oriented randomization methodology），根据输入数据的底层生成机制自适应调整随机化策略，特别针对非结构化复杂数据。在此基础上构建稳定性保障理论框架，并将方法扩展至受大语言模型架构启发的top-k排序问题。

Result: 建立了严谨的稳定性理论基础，详细刻画了稳定性与探索之间的内在权衡关系。通过大量数值模拟和真实世界数据集应用验证了方法的有效性和可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应随机化框架能有效应对黑盒模型在多样化输入下的稳定性挑战，在理论与实践层面均取得了积极成果，为构建可信人工智能提供了新路径。

Abstract: As black-box models become foundational to modern research, ensuring their stability is paramount for the realization of trustworthy artificial intelligence. The inherent diversity of inputs - ranging from structured Gaussian distributions to complex data with unknown structures - poses a significant challenge: how to stabilize black-box outputs while effectively leveraging available prior information. This paper introduces a task-oriented randomization methodology that adaptively tailors its strategy to the underlying generative mechanisms of the input data, specifically addressing unstructured complexities. A comprehensive suite of stability guarantees is proposed. Beyond establishing rigorous theoretical foundations for stability, the research provides a detailed analysis of the intrinsic trade-off between stability and exploration. Motivated by the architecture of Large Language Models, the framework is further extended to top-k ranking problems. The validity and effectiveness of the proposal are demonstrated through extensive numerical simulations and applications to the real-world dataset.

</details>

Conclusion: LTT范式为超参数选择提供了一个具备正式统计保证的统一框架，显著提升了AI系统部署中超参数选择的可靠性和安全性，克服了传统经验方法的局限。

Abstract: Hyperparameter selection is a critical step in the deployment of modern artificial intelligence systems, given the need to tune degrees of freedom such as inference-time parameters, implementation-level settings, and thresholds driving decision rules. Despite its practical importance, hyperparameter selection is typically performed using best-effort empirical methods such as grid search or Bayesian optimization, which provide no formal statistical guarantees on reliability or safety.
  This monograph presents a unified statistical framework for reliable hyperparameter selection, centered on the learn-then-test (LTT) paradigm, which formulates the problem as multiple hypothesis testing over a candidate set of hyperparameters. The framework enables the selection of hyperparameters that provably satisfy application-specific reliability requirements -- such as bounds on average risk, quantile risk, or information-theoretic constraints -- with explicit, finite-sample control of error probabilities. The supporting statistical machinery, namely p-values, e-values, and concentration inequalities, is developed from first principles in a dedicated appendix.

</details>


### [23] [Gaussian Mean Field Variational Inference can Overestimate Predictive Variance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25745)
*James Odgers,Ben Riegler,Siddharth Swaroop,Vincent Fortuin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了MFVI在贝叶斯线性回归中一个反直觉的现象：虽然MFVI在参数空间低估后验方差，但在训练数据密集的方向上会高估预测方差，导致对分布内测试点的预期预测方差超过精确后验。作者将这一现象与"冷后验效应"联系起来，提出调整温度可以修正这种高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为MFVI总是低估后验方差，但这一刻画并不完整。作者旨在揭示MFVI在预测方差方面被忽视的行为——即在某些方向上会高估预测方差，并探讨这一现象对预测性能的影响及其与冷后验效应的关联。

Method: 通过对共轭贝叶斯线性回归的解析分析，分解预测方差为参数空间的不同方向，证明MFVI在训练数据集中方向高估预测方差而在其他方向低估的必然性。进一步理论推导了分布内数据的预期预测方差性质，并通过合成和真实回归任务进行实验验证。

Result: MFVI在参数空间低估方差，但在训练数据集中方向上必然高估预测方差；对来自训练分布的测试点，MFVI的预期预测方差超过精确后验；极端情况下MFVI后验无法相对先验降低预测方差；通过温度参数调节可以修正这种高估，使预测更接近精确后验。

Conclusion: MFVI对后验方差的刻画需要重新审视：其在预测方差上的高估行为是结构性的、不可避免的，且集中在数据密集方向。这一发现为理解冷后验效应提供了新视角——调节温度实质上是对MFVI预测方差高估的矫正。

Abstract: Mean Field Variational Inference (MFVI) is widely understood to underestimate posterior variance. By analysing conjugate Bayesian Linear Regression (BLR), we show that this characterization is incomplete: while MFVI underestimates the variance in parameter space, it can overestimate the predictive variance compared to the exact posterior. We show that if the MFVI posterior underestimates predictive variances in some directions, it necessarily overestimates them in others. Crucially, this overestimation occurs in directions where the training data concentrates. This leads to the surprising result that, for a test point drawn from the training distribution, MFVI's expected predictive variance exceeds that of the exact posterior. We demonstrate a pathological case of this effect, where the MFVI posterior fails to reduce predictive variance compared to the prior on in distribution data. We connect these results to the Cold Posterior Effect, arguing that varying the temperature can correct this overestimation, yielding predictions closer to those of the exact posterior. We validate our theory on synthetic and real-world regression tasks.

</details>


### [24] [FedReLa: Imbalanced Federated Learning via Re-Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26037)
*Guangzheng Hu,Patricia Menéndez,Feng Liu,Mingming Gong,Guanghui Wang,Liuhua Peng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: FedReLa 是一种联邦学习数据级方法，通过特征依赖的标签重分配器修正全局决策边界偏差，无需知晓全局类别分布，在数据异构与类别不平衡共存场景下显著提升少数类及整体准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中全局类别不平衡与跨客户端数据异构性共存，局部与全局不平衡的错配加剧模型性能下降。现有数据级方法依赖全局类别分布先验，在极端类别缺失场景下失效，亟需一种不依赖该先验的解决方案。

Method: 提出 FedReLa，一种模型无关的模块化数据级方法。核心机制是通过特征依赖的标签重分配器（feature-dependent label re-allocator）对样本进行重新标注，在无需全局类别分布知识的前提下修正有偏的全局决策边界。该方法可与现有算法方法集成，不引入额外通信开销。

Result: 在阶梯式不平衡（stepwise-imbalanced）和长尾（long-tailed）数据集上的广泛实验表明，FedReLa 显著提升了少数类的准确率以及整体准确率，性能优于此前的最先进方法。

Conclusion: FedReLa 有效解决了联邦学习下数据异构与类别不平衡的共存问题，以模型无关、无需全局分布先验、无额外通信代价的方式实现鲁棒的全局决策边界修正，展现出优越的实用性和扩展性。

Abstract: Federated learning has emerged as the foremost approach for decentralized model training with privacy preservation. The global class imbalance and cross-client data heterogeneity naturally coexist, and the mismatch between local and global imbalances exacerbates the performance degradation of the aggregated model. The agnosticism of global class distribution poses significant challenges for data-level methods, especially under extreme conditions with severe class absence across clients. In this paper, we propose FedReLa, a novel data-level approach that tackles the coexistence of data heterogeneity and class imbalance in federated learning. By re-labeling samples with a feature-dependent label re-allocator, FedReLa corrects biased global decision boundaries without requiring knowledge of the global class distribution. This modular, model-agnostic approach can be integrated with algorithmic methods to deliver consistent improvements without additional communication overhead. Through extensive experiments, our method significantly improves the accuracy of minority classes and the overall accuracy on stepwise-imbalanced and long-tailed datasets, outperforming the previous state of the art.

</details>

*Zhengchi Ma,Pengfei Lyu,Anru R. Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从理论角度分析了合成数据增强对基于分数的二分类模型的影响，将其分解为有效类别权重变化和合成分布与真实分布的差异两个组成部分。在模型设定正确时，增强无法带来超越原始估计量的群体层面改进；但在模型设定错误时，增强可通过改变有效类别平衡来修正排序错误，并提供了显式的改进界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据增强被广泛用于缓解类别不平衡问题，但其对基于分数的分类器的理论影响尚不清晰。现有实践缺乏对何时增强能真正改善AUROC、AUPRC、平衡准确率和F1等指标的系统性理论理解。

Method: 建立统一的理论框架，将增强效应分解为有效类别权重变化和合成-真实分布差异两部分。在模型设定正确和错误两种情形下，分别分析增强对阈值积分型（AUROC、AUPRC）和阈值优化型（最佳阈值平衡准确率、F1）指标的影响；利用极小极大下界证明原始估计量的最优性；在设定错误时给出基于近似误差、有限样本估计误差和合成分布误差的显式改进界限。

Result: 在模型设定正确时，原始估计量已针对似然比排序，达到群体最优和极小极大最优的指标遗憾率，增强只能提供有限的有限样本方差缩减，且可能引入合成分布偏差。在模型设定错误时，增强通过改变有效类别权重来改变受限类别投影，可修正原始不平衡目标导致的排序错误。仿真实验验证了理论：设定正确时收益有限，设定错误时存在非平凡但非单调的改进。

Conclusion: 合成少数类增强的效果高度依赖于模型设定是否正确：设定正确时增强价值有限，设定错误时增强可通过重新加权机制提供实质性改进。这一发现为实践中何时以及如何有效使用合成数据增强提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Synthetic data augmentation is widely used to mitigate class imbalance, but its theoretical effects on score-based classification remain poorly understood. This paper develops a framework for characterizing when synthetic minority augmentation can improve threshold-integrated and threshold-optimized metrics, including AUROC, AUPRC, best-threshold balanced accuracy, and best-threshold \(\F_1\) score. We separate the effect of augmentation into two components: a change in effective class weighting and a discrepancy between the synthetic and true minority distributions. Under well-specified score models, the raw estimator already targets the likelihood-ratio ordering, which is population-optimal for the metrics considered. Consequently, augmentation cannot provide a fundamental population-level improvement beyond possible finite-sample variance reduction, and may introduce additional bias through synthetic distributional error. We further establish minimax lower bounds showing that the raw estimator already achieves the optimal metric-regret rate in the well-specified regime. Under misspecification, however, augmentation can play a qualitatively different role: by changing the effective class balance, it can alter the restricted-class projection and correct ranking errors induced by the raw imbalanced objective. We provide explicit improvement bounds quantifying the roles of approximation error, finite-sample estimation error, and synthetic distributional error. Simulation studies corroborate the theory, demonstrating limited gains under well-specification and nontrivial but nonmonotone improvements under misspecification.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 开发了一个R包，核心采用计算机视觉模型对残差图进行自动化评估。模型接收残差样本后，输出一个量化的"视觉信号强度"（Visual Signal Strength, VSS）指标，并附带辅助诊断信息。同时构建了Shiny交互式网页应用作为前端界面，降低用户使用门槛。

Result: 该工具能够自动预测残差图的视觉信号强度（VSS），为分析师评估模型拟合优度提供客观、可重复的量化参考，从而减少人工评估的不一致性。

Conclusion: 通过计算机视觉模型实现残差图诊断的自动化是可行的，该R包及Shiny应用为线性模型诊断提供了一种可扩展、客观且易用的自动评估方案，有望替代传统的人工目视判读流程。

Abstract: Visual assessment of residual plots is a common approach for diagnosing linear models, but it relies on manual evaluation, which does not scale well and can lead to inconsistent decisions across analysts. The lineup protocol, which embeds the observed plot among null plots, can reduce subjectivity but requires even more human effort. In today's data-driven world, such tasks are well suited for automation. We present a new R package that uses a computer vision model to automate the evaluation of residual plots. An accompanying Shiny application is provided for ease of use. Given a sample of residuals, the model predicts a visual signal strength (VSS) and offers supporting information to help analysts assess model fit.

</details>


### [27] [Model selection with proper scoring rules on data sets of time series: prefer the mean scaled score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24715)
*Giorgio Corani,Stefano Damato,Dario Azzimonti,Lorenzo Zambon*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究多时间序列数据集上概率预测模型的模型选择问题，揭示了不同聚合准则（均值缩放得分、平均排名、胜率等）产生冲突结论的根源在于得分分布的偏态性，并证明均值得分对此免疫，且随测试集增大所有准则趋于一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多时间序列的概率预测模型评估中，常用的聚合准则（均值缩放得分、平均排名、胜率等）常给出相互矛盾的模型选择结果。作者旨在探究这种矛盾的根本原因，并确定最可靠的模型选择方法。

Method: 通过理论分析得分分布的偏态性（skewness）对不同聚合准则的影响：证明均值得分不受偏态影响，而非均值准则（中位数、排名、胜率等）会因偏态而选择错误设定的模型。进一步证明随测试集长度增加，所有准则的决策渐近收敛。在间歇性需求时间序列（含M5竞赛数据）上进行实证验证。

Result: 1) 得分分布的偏态性是非均值准则选择错误模型的原因；2) 均值得分对该问题免疫；3) 随测试集增大，所有聚合准则收敛到相同决策；4) 均值缩放得分在实证中最为可靠，且对不同缩放因子具有决策一致性。

Conclusion: 在多时间序列模型选择中，应优先采用均值缩放得分（mean scaled score），并确保使用足够大的测试集以规避因得分偏态导致的模型误选风险。均值缩放得分的决策对缩放因子的选择也具有稳健性。

Abstract: We study the problem of model selection among probabilistic forecasting models evaluated on datasets of multiple time series. The performance of a model on a single time series is quantified by the average value (score) of a proper scoring rule over a test set, but extending model selection to data sets of time series requires aggregating these scores. Common approaches either rely on scaling scores and averaging them (mean scaled score) or avoid scaling by using alternative statistics such as mean ranks or win rates. However, these approaches can yield conflicting conclusions. We show that such discrepancies arise from the skewness of the distribution of the scores, which is particularly pronounced when test sets are short. The skewness can cause non-mean criteria (e.g., mean rank, median, win rate) to select misspecified models. In contrast, the mean score is immune from this problem. We further show that, as the size of the test sets increases, all aggregation criteria converge to the same model selection decision, mitigating these discrepancies. Our experiments on intermittent demand time series, including data from the M5 competition, highlight the importance of sufficiently large test sets; the mean scaled score appears to be the more reliable approach, also because empirically we found its decision to remain consistent when different scaling factors are adopted.

</details>

### [28] [Critique of Agent Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23991)
*Eric Xing,Mingkai Deng,Jinyu Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从笛卡尔的自主思想理论出发，结合科幻作品中对自主存在的描绘，系统区分了"agentic"（工程化工作流驱动）与"agentive"（能力内生涌现）两类系统，提出真正的能动性需具备目标、身份、决策、自我调节与学习五个维度的内部化结构，并设计了面向通用智能体的GIC（目标-身份-配置器）架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM驱动的"编码智能体""AI联合科学家"等工具兴起，以及同时存在的AI失控、机器能动性等存在性忧虑，亟需厘清自动化与能动性的边界——既为构建可靠系统，也为理性评估风险。

Method: 以笛卡尔关于能动性根植于独立思想的哲学框架和科幻作品为分析起点，调研当前AI智能体景观，沿目标、身份、决策、自我调节、学习五个维度剖析智能体架构，提出"agentic vs. agentive"的核心区分标准，并据此构建GIC（Goal-Identity-Configurator）通用智能体架构，融合层次化目标分解、身份演化、基于独立世界模型的模拟推理、习得性自我调节及虚实结合的自主学习。

Result: 明确了"agentic系统"（能力源于外部工程化工作流编排）与"agentive系统"（包括社交能力在内的各项能力内生涌现）的根本差异，后者才是真正具备开放世界自主运作能力的系统；GIC架构为构建此类通用智能体提供了可行蓝图。

Conclusion: 真正的能动性要求五大结构内化于系统自身而非依赖外部脚手架；agentive系统虽拥有更高自主性与"能动性"但仍可置于人类监督之下，论文还就可审计性、可控性与安全性给出了见解。

Abstract: What is an agent? What constitutes agency? With the rise of Large Language Model (LLM) systems marketed as ``coding agents'', ``AI co-scientists'', and other ``agentic" tools that promise to drive up productivity, and at the same time, ``existential" concerns such as AI escaping human control with destructive power under a speculative ``machine agency" against humans, it has become essential to clarify where automation ends and agency begins, both for building capable systems and for understanding whether and what to fear. Drawing on Descartes' grounding of agency in independent thought, and on portrayals of autonomous beings in science fiction, we survey the current landscape of AI agents, and analyze agent architectures along five dimensions: goal, identity, decision-making, self-regulation, and learning. Specifically, we argue that genuine agency requires these structures to be \emph{internalized within the system itself} rather than assembled through external scaffolding. This distinction between \emph{agentic} systems, whose competence resides in engineered workflows, and \emph{agentive} systems, whose capabilities (including social interaction) arise endogenously, defines the boundary between systems designed for prescribed tasks, and those capable of operating in the open world with true autonomy. Building on this analysis, we propose the Goal-Identity-Configurator (GIC) architecture for a general-purpose agent model, combining hierarchical goal decomposition, identity evolution, simulative reasoning grounded in a separately trained world model, learned self-regulation, and self-directed learning from both real and simulated experience. Furthermore, we share insight on the auditability, controllability, and safety of agentive systems that possess greater autonomy and ``agency", but remain under human oversight.

</details>


### [29] [Ensemble Feature Selection and Harris Hawks Optimization for Explainable Mental Health Risk Prediction in Female Sex Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24047)
*Ahnaf Atef Choudhury,Md. Parvej Hoque Palash,Shahriar Siddique Ayon,Ramkrishna Saha,Abdullah Al Mamun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合预测模型，结合ANOVA与互信息的集成特征选择以及哈里斯鹰优化算法调优的逻辑回归，用于预测女性性工作者（FSWs）的抑郁症风险，准确率达95.78%，AUC为0.96，并通过可解释AI方法识别创伤后应激、客户相关暴力和职业因素为主要风险因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 女性性工作者面临暴力、污名化和经济困难等多重风险因素，抑郁症高发。然而现有机器学习模型难以有效捕捉该边缘群体中高维且复杂的风险模式，导致预测能力不足，亟需更精准且可解释的预测工具以支持早期干预。

Method: 提出一种混合预测模型，包含三个核心组件：(1) 集成特征选择策略，结合ANOVA方差分析和互信息（Mutual Information）筛选关键特征；(2) 使用哈里斯鹰优化算法（Harris Hawks Optimization, HHO）对逻辑回归模型进行超参数调优；(3) 引入可解释AI（XAI）方法揭示模型预测背后的创伤相关因素。模型在3,005名FSWs样本上验证。

Result: 所提模型显著优于传统分类器，准确率95.78%，F1分数95.77%，AUC达0.96。XAI分析识别出创伤后应激、客户相关暴力及职业因素为抑郁症的主要贡献因子。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了传统方法与机器学习方法之间的鸿沟，开发了一款可解释AI工具，能够帮助边缘群体获得早期援助和基于证据的靶向社会心理护理，为相关健康规划提供决策支持。

Abstract: One of the significant mental health issues affecting female sex workers (FSWs) is mental disorders, especially depression. Exposure to violence, stigma, and economic hardship further increases their psychological risk. Current machine learning (ML) models are typically ineffective at capturing the high-dimensional and complex risk patterns that exist in this marginalized group. The explainable AI (XAI) methods can be used to understand the factors of trauma associated with model predictions. When applied to a group of 3,005 FSWs, it can be seen that the proposed model is more effective than traditional classifiers, with an accuracy of 95.78%, an F1 score of 95.77%, and an AUC of 0.96, and identifying post-traumatic stress, client-related violence, and occupational factors as major contributors to depression. Abstract: We introduce a large-scale, open-source dataset of pre-generated adversarial attacks for vision-language models (VLMs). The dataset is designed to be diverse, representative, and practical, extending existing benchmarks by covering 10 high-level categories and 55 subcategories of harmful intents. Our primary goal is to make adversarial data accessible to the research community, given the computational cost and complexity of generating large numbers of attacks. The dataset comprises 47 524 adversarial samples, generated using state-of-the-art attack strategies from recent literature. Our work complements existing efforts by consolidating and extending prior benchmarks from multiple established sources, resulting in 7 826 intents, and introduce an additional category to broaden coverage. This provides realistic evaluation resources for studying model robustness and alignment. Our dataset intends to enable researchers and practitioners to systematically evaluate the robustness and safety of VLMs, fine-tune attack-generation models, and develop or stress-test defensive guardrails under diverse adversarial conditions. By releasing this resource, we aim to lower the barrier to adversarial research and foster more reproducible, comprehensive, and comparable evaluations of VLM safety.

TL;DR: ASALT方法通过在观察层面和状态层面引入适配器，将源域与目标域的不同维度映射到共享嵌入空间，从而在异构MARL域间实现高效策略迁移，并在协作环境中从采样效率和全局回报上超越了现有基线。

Method: 提出ASALT（Actor-State Adapter Transfer）方法，在actor和critic中分别嵌入观察级适配器和状态级适配器，将目标域的观察与全局状态映射到与源域对齐的共享嵌入空间，从而兼容异构域间的策略与价值函数迁移。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) addresses the problem of training multiple agents that pursue collaborative, competitive, or mixed objectives. Prior work has investigated transfer learning between source and target domains in MARL; however, the majority of existing approaches impose the constraint that the dimensionalities of the observation space and the global state space must be identical across domains. In this paper, we introduce a method that explicitly accommodates mismatched state-space dimensionalities between source and target domains. The proposed approach, ASALT, incorporates both observation-level and state-level adapters that map the target-domain observations and global states into a shared embedding space, thereby enabling more effective transfer of knowledge across both actors and critics. These adapters can generate embeddings that support efficient strategy transfer across heterogeneous domains. Experimental results on multiple configurations in standard benchmark environments demonstrate that ASALT surpasses existing baselines in terms of sample efficiency and global return in cooperative settings, but its effectiveness depends on the degree of mismatch between source and target domains. Furthermore, our findings indicate that ASALT mitigates negative transfer, which frequently constitutes a major obstacle when transferring policies between domains with differing observation and action spaces.

</details>

*Dongqing Li,Geoff K. Nicholls,Jeong Eun Lee,Chuxuan,Jiang*

TL;DR: 本文提出层次偏序（HPO）模型，将基于偏序的排名聚合方法扩展到分组数据场景，通过潜在偏序层次结构实现组间信息共享。Plackett-Luce模型及其层次变体被证明是HPO的特例。进一步提出HCPO用于无监督聚类。贝叶斯推断通过MCMC实现，在合成与真实数据上均优于现有方法。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出HPO模型，通过潜在偏序层次结构扩展poset基模型以适应分组数据，类似Mallows/Plackett-Luce的混合模型扩展。进一步提出HCPO（层次聚类偏序）扩展，在组标签未知时实现无监督聚类。使用MCMC方法进行贝叶斯推断，对潜在偏序层次进行后验采样。

Result: 实验表明HPO和HCPO模型在合成数据和真实数据集（包括成对声学偏好数据和大语言模型智能体轨迹数据）上，在预测性能和结构可解释性两方面均优于现有方法。Plackett-Luce及其层次变体被证明为HPO的特殊情形。

Conclusion: HPO框架成功统一了偏序建模与分组结构，在保持偏序结构的同时实现组间信息的原则性共享，为具有异质性群体的偏好数据分析提供了新工具，兼具预测能力和可解释性。

Abstract: Rank aggregation combines information from ordered lists ranking items by preference. Classical parametric models for such data, including the Mallows and Plackett-Luce models, assume the orders concentrate around one or more complete consensus rankings. Recent work relaxes the total-order assumption by allowing the consensus structure to be a partial order (poset), allowing for incomparabilities in preferences. However, in many applications preference data exhibit group structure. We introduce hierarchical partial order (HPO) models, which extend poset-based models to accommodate grouped data through a hierarchy of latent posets. This framework, which parallels mixture model extensions of the Mallows and Plackett-Luce models, enables principled sharing of information across groups while preserving partial-order structure. We show that the Plackett-Luce model and its hierarchical variants are special cases of HPO-models. We develop a hierarchical clustering extension (HCPO) for unsupervised clustering in settings where group labels are unknown. Bayesian inference for the latent poset hierarchy is performed using Markov chain Monte Carlo methods. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets, including pairwise acoustic preference data and LLM agent traces, demonstrate that the proposed HPO and HCPO models outperform existing approaches in both predictive performance and structural interpretability.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种计算高效的时空分解方法，在行政边界随时间变化的场景下，从观测计数数据中恢复高分辨率风险面。方法扩展了空间聚合对数高斯Cox过程，采用扩展潜高斯模型框架进行快速近似后验推断，并用伽马分布的过度离散替代传统对数正态多边形效应，从而获得边际负二项似然并消除每对多边形-时间点的潜变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疾病制图和小区域估计中，报告单元的地理边界经常随时间变化（如行政区划调整），传统方法难以处理这种边界漂移问题。需要在边界变化条件下实现高分辨率风险面的恢复，同时保证计算效率。

Method: 扩展空间聚合对数高斯Cox过程（log-Gaussian Cox process），将其嵌入扩展潜高斯模型（Extended Latent Gaussian Model）框架以实现快速近似后验推断。关键创新在于：用伽马分布（gamma-distributed）的过度离散项替代传统对数正态分布的多边形特异性随机效应，使边际似然退化为负二项分布，从而消除每对多边形-时间单元的潜在变量，大幅降低计算维度。

Result: 在比利时和荷兰NUTS-3边界变迁的死亡率制图案例中验证了方法的有效性。此外，利用Codex平台将该方法应用于曼彻斯特市的独立数据集进行推广。方法已实现为开源R包DAST。

Conclusion: 所提出的时空分解方法能够在报告边界动态变化条件下有效恢复高分辨率风险面，计算效率显著优于传统方法，并通过开源软件包DAST实现，具备良好的可复现性和推广价值。

Abstract: Small area estimation and disease mapping increasingly rely on areal data where reporting boundaries change over time. We develop a computationally efficient spatio-temporal disaggregation method to recover high-resolution risk surfaces from observed counts under changing boundaries. Our approach extends the spatially aggregated log-Gaussian Cox process and uses the Extended Latent Gaussian Model framework for fast approximate posterior inference. We replace standard lognormal polygon-specific effects with gamma-distributed overdispersion which yields a marginal negative binomial likelihood, and removes one latent variable per polygon-time pair. We illustrate the approach by mapping mortality risk across shifting NUTS-3 boundaries in Belgium and the Netherlands. For the purpose of dissemination we use Codex to leverage the methodology presented in this paper for the analysis of a separate data set concerning the city of Manchester. The methodology is implemented in the open-source R package DAST.

</details>

*Jack M. Wolf,Nandita Mitra,Ashkan Ertefaie*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非参数高效估计方法，用于在具有大量决策点的即时自适应干预（JITAI）研究中，估计针对远期结局的随机策略方案-反应曲线，并通过数据自适应倾斜程序稳定估计，同时为最优随机策略的推断提供统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动和可穿戴技术使得JITAI成为可能，但估计最优JITAI面临挑战：现有方法在个体拥有数十个决策点的情境下，估计量不稳定、不规则，存在显著偏差且收敛速度慢；先进强化学习方法难以解释，且通常聚焦于近端折扣结果而非定义长期成功的远期结局。

Method: 1. 在一类随机策略下，针对远期结局开发方案-反应曲线的非参数高效估计量；2. 引入数据自适应倾斜（tilting）程序，在多决策点情境下稳定估计；3. 证明估计的方案-反应曲线弱收敛于高斯过程，构建同时置信带；4. 推导曲线优化器的渐近理论，实现最优随机策略的统计推断。

Result: 所提出的方案-反应曲线估计量具有非参数效率；曲线弱收敛于高斯过程，可用于构建同时置信带；优化器的渐近理论使得对学习到的最优随机策略进行推断成为可能。

Conclusion: 该研究为面向远期结局的随机JITAI的估计、推断和优化提供了一个统一框架，解决了现有方法在大量决策点情境下的不稳定性、偏差和推断困难等问题。

Abstract: Mobile and wearable technologies enable the delivery of just-in-time adaptive interventions (JITAIs) -- interventions that adapt treatment delivery to an individual's rapidly changing internal state and context in real-time, real-world settings. Estimating optimal JITAIs, however, remains challenging because these studies often involve dozens of decision points per individual, and existing methods can produce unstable and irregular estimators with substantial bias and slow convergence rates. Advanced reinforcement learning approaches may be difficult to interpret and often target proximal, discounted outcomes rather than the distal end-of-study outcomes that define long-term success in many behavioral and clinical studies. To address these challenges, we develop a nonparametrically efficient estimator of the regimen-response curve for distal outcomes under a class of stochastic policies and introduce a data-adaptive tilting procedure to stabilize estimation in settings with many decision points. We show that the estimated regimen-response curve converges weakly to a Gaussian process, enabling simultaneous confidence bands, and we derive asymptotic theory for the optimizer of the curve, thereby enabling inference for the learned optimal stochastic policy. These developments provide a unified framework for estimation, inference, and optimization of stochastic JITAIs for distal outcomes.

</details>
