*Riccardo De Santis,Annamaria Guolo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Beta回归模型的多重插补框架，用于处理双变量与替代Meta分析中部分缺失的相关性数据，在MAR假设下优于完全案例分析及简单均值插补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双变量和替代Meta分析中，相关性数据常部分缺失。完全案例分析虽简单，但会导致估计效率大幅下降和潜在偏倚。现有方法要么依赖简化假设下的似然框架进行聚合估计，要么采用确定性插补（如经验均值），均未能充分处理MAR机制下的缺失问题。

Method: 提出基于Beta回归模型的多重插补（Multiple Imputation）框架，通过随机过程进行插补，显式建模缺失机制中的MAR假设。Beta回归模型用于刻画相关性数据的取值范围（通常有界于一定区间）及其与观测协变量的关系，从而生成合理的插补值并进行多重推断。

Result: 通过广泛的模拟研究，在多种场景下将所提方法与简单均值插补及完全案例分析进行对比，结果表明所提多冲插补方法在MAR假设下具有更优的表现。

Conclusion: 基于Beta回归模型的多重插补方法能有效处理双变量与替代Meta分析中的部分缺失相关性数据，在MAR假设下优于现有简单方法，为实践者提供了更可靠的统计分析工具。

Abstract: This work addresses the issue of partially missing correlations within the framework of bivariate and surrogate meta-analyses. While restricting the analysis to complete-case studies may appear to constitute the most straightforward analytical strategy, such an approach has been demonstrated to yield substantial inefficiencies and potential bias in the resulting estimates. Current methodological contributions in the literature circumvent this limitation either through aggregate estimation procedures grounded in likelihood-based frameworks under simplifying assumptions, or by resorting to deterministic imputation strategies, such as the empirical mean derived from observed units. In the present paper, we propose a multiple imputation framework in which imputation is performed via stochastic procedures based on a Beta regression model, thereby explicitly accounting for the missing at random (MAR) assumption underlying the observed missingness mechanism. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method through an extensive simulation study across various scenarios, comparing our proposal with simple mean imputation and complete-case analysis under the MAR assumption.

</details>


### [36] [Imprecise Transition Matrices for Markov Cohort Models: Lower and Upper Expectations with a Practical Health Economic Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25716)
*Rowan Iskandar*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不精确概率扩展的马尔可夫队列模型，用于当可用证据（转移计数、结构约束、治疗效果数据）仅能识别一组可接受转移矩阵而非唯一矩阵时，计算有限时间范围内累积结局的下期望和上期望。通过Bellman式上下转移算子实现精确计算，并证明了包络定理、还原性、相干性等理论性质。以卵圆孔未闭封堵术的成本效果分析为例，展示了该方法如何识别证据未充分解决时决策对转移概率的非稳健依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在应用健康研究中，马尔可夫队列模型通常依赖唯一指定的转移概率矩阵，但实际可用证据（转移计数、结构约束、治疗效果数据）往往只能确定一组可接受的矩阵集合，而非唯一合理矩阵。现有不精确马尔可夫链研究未聚焦于有限水平队列轨迹、加性累积结局及基于经验转移计数构建的转移矩阵。因此需要一种在不精确概率框架下，对证据兼容的精确马尔可夫队列模型集进行推断的方法。

Method: 将马尔可夫队列模型拓展至不精确概率框架：在非空紧致且分离指定的出边行集合（outgoing-row sets）条件下，通过Bellman式下转移算子和上转移算子精确计算累积结局的下期望和上期望。推导并证明了包络定理、向经典模型的还原性、下转移算子的相干性，以及单一选定矩阵导致非稳健决策的代数条件。利用不精确Dirichlet模型（Imprecise Dirichlet Model）将多项转移计数转化为可接受矩阵集。通过卵圆孔未闭封堵术的真实成本效果案例进行实证检验。

Result: 理论层面：证明了包络定理、向经典模型的还原、下转移算子的相干性，并给出了单一矩阵导致非稳健决策的代数条件。实证层面：在卵圆孔未闭封堵术后隐源性卒中的成本效果分析中，经验转移矩阵略微倾向封堵术，但不精确分析得到的增量净货币收益区间跨越零点，表明决策对尚未被证据完全解决的转移概率存在非稳健依赖。

Conclusion: 该方法既提供了严格的下期望公式体系，也为那些依赖于未被证据充分解决的转移概率的决策提供了一种实用的诊断工具。不精确分析能够揭示传统精确模型可能掩盖的决策非稳健性。

Abstract: In applied health research, Markov cohort models are built on a precisely specified transition probability matrix. However, in many applications, the available evidence -- transition counts, structural constraints, and treatment-effect data -- identifies a set of admissible matrices rather than one uniquely justified matrix. This paper formulates an imprecise-probability extension in which inference yields lower and upper expectations over an evidence-compatible set of precise Markov cohort models. The contribution differs from existing imprecise Markov-chain work by focusing on finite-horizon cohort trajectories, additive accumulated outcomes, and transition matrices constructed from empirical transition counts. Under non-empty compact separately specified outgoing-row sets, the lower and upper accumulated outcomes are computed exactly by Bellman-style lower and upper transition operators. We prove the envelope theorem, reduction to the classical model, coherence properties of the lower transition operator, and algebraic conditions under which a single selected matrix yields a non-robust decision. We then show how multinomial transition counts induce admissible matrix sets through the Imprecise Dirichlet Model. A real-world cost-effectiveness example of patent foramen ovale closure after cryptogenic stroke illustrates the practical consequence: the empirical transition matrix slightly favors closure, whereas the imprecise analysis yields an incremental net monetary benefit interval crossing zero. The method provides both a rigorous lower-expectation formulation and a practical diagnostic for decisions that depend on transition probabilities not fully resolved by the evidence.

</details>


### [37] [A Theory of Bootstrap Coverage Calibration for Generalized Posterior Credible Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25729)
*Masahiro Tanaka*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 广义后验用指数化经验准则替代似然函数，但其可信集通常缺乏频率学派覆盖的渐近保证。本文提出通过Bootstrap估计覆盖率来校准标量学习率，利用Edgeworth展开推导高阶覆盖展开式，并分析了算法中的随机近似步骤。研究表明，Bootstrap校准是一种特定于显著性水平的尺度修正，而非对一般形状误设的补救。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义后验分布的可信集在频率学派意义下通常缺乏覆盖率的渐近保证，需要一种方法来校准学习率以提高覆盖率的准确性。现有方法缺乏对校准机制的高阶理论分析。

Method: 基于正则固定维渐近框架下的Edgeworth展开，推导可信集覆盖率的高阶展开式；分析Bootstrap覆盖方程根的渐近性质；将覆盖误差分解为估计量的采样Edgeworth修正与后验Edgeworth修正两个来源；分析随机近似步骤的收敛性。

Result: 在均匀覆盖近似和局部识别条件下，Bootstrap覆盖方程根具有一致性。高阶展开揭示了覆盖误差的两个来源：估计量的采样Edgeworth修正，以及可信集边界、中心和形状的后验Edgeworth修正。标量学习率仅在后验协方差与采样协方差成比例时（即高斯极限情况）才能校准所有显著性水平，否则Bootstrap校准仅为特定水平的尺度修正。

Conclusion: Bootstrap校准本质上是特定显著性水平上的尺度修正，无法解决一般的形状误设问题。只有当后验协方差与采样协方差成比例时，单一标量学习率才能同时校准所有名义水平。

Abstract: Generalized posteriors replace the likelihood by an exponentiated empirical criterion, but their credible sets generally lack asymptotic justification for frequentist coverage. General posterior calibration selects a scalar learning rate by estimating coverage with the bootstrap. Using Edgeworth expansions under regular fixed-dimensional asymptotics, we derive higher-order coverage expansions and analyze the stochastic approximation step used in the implemented algorithm. For a fixed nominal level, the root of the bootstrap coverage equation is consistent under a uniform coverage approximation and local identification. The higher-order expansions separate two sources of coverage error: the sampling Edgeworth correction for the estimator and the posterior Edgeworth correction for credible set boundaries, centres, and shapes. A scalar learning rate can calibrate all nominal levels in the Gaussian limit only when the posterior covariance and the sampling covariance are proportional. Hence, bootstrap calibration is a level-specific scale correction, not a remedy for general shape misspecification.

</details>

Abstract: Longitudinal data collected in clinical trials are almost always incomplete due to some of the participants dropping out from the study during the planned follow-up. A common strategy to handle nonresponse expresses missingness in terms of a dropout process, which is jointly analysed with the outcome process to facilitate the formulation of the missingness assumptions. However, when the outcome is multivariate, the identification of the dropout process becomes problematic, especially when individuals have different dropout times for each type of response, and sensitivity analysis is difficult. The modelling task may be also be complicated by data complexities (e.g. skewness and spikes) which are difficult to capture through standard parametric methods. An example of this analysis framework occurs in trial-based economic evaluations, where a longitudinal bivariate response, formed by suitably-defined measures of effectiveness and costs, is analysed to inform policymakers about the cost-effectiveness of alternative interventions. We present a novel Bayesian nonparametric approach to handle a missing bivariate longitudinal outcome by jointly modelling the dropout process associated with each type of response while also taking into account the complexities of the data. We specify a flexible nonparametric model for the observed data and partially identify the distribution of the missing data with identifying restrictions conditional on the dropout indicators and sensitivity parameters. We explore alternative nonignorable scenarios through different priors for the sensitivity parameters. Our approach is motivated by, and applied to, data from a trial assessing the cost-effectiveness of a new treatment for intellectual disability and challenging behaviour.

Abstract: As machine learning models and datasets continue to grow, developing complex models has become increasingly computationally demanding. Knowledge distillation reduces deployment cost by compressing a large, well-trained teacher model into a compact student model, but it does not address settings where constructing the teacher itself is the bottleneck. Motivated by this challenge, we introduce Knowledge Cascade (KCas), a reverse knowledge distillation framework that uses information from a small, inexpensive student model to guide the development of a more complex teacher model. Although this direction is counterintuitive because the teacher typically has greater representational capacity, we show that student-to-teacher transfer can be principled when supported by statistical scaling relationships. We first develop KCas for nonparametric multivariate functional estimation in reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces via smoothing splines, where selecting multiple smoothing parameters is a major computational bottleneck. KCas transfers student-selected smoothing parameters to the full-sample regime through asymptotic scaling laws, substantially reducing computational cost for high-dimensional and large-scale datasets while retaining theoretical guarantees. Beyond smoothing splines, we illustrate the same principle through kernel density estimation and deep learning hyperparameter transfer. Simulations and real-data experiments show that KCas achieves substantial computational savings while maintaining strong statistical performance, and can sometimes outperform the corresponding full-sample procedure.

</details>

Method: 提出ERHT-CC检验：首先，利用样本空间中位数和以其为中心的空间符号协方差矩阵构建正则化Hotelling检验统计量；其次，推导其零假设渐近正态性及中心化/方差的一致估计量；然后，通过在确定性网格上聚合不同固定岭参数下的p值，利用Cauchy组合规则得到最终检验p值，无需估计交叉岭参数相关性；最后，建立有限网格联合高斯极限，给出解析组合p值并刻画其局部功效。

Conclusion: ERHT-CC为高维厚尾及强相关数据下的单样本位置检验提供了理论完备、计算可行的解决方案，其基于空间符号的鲁棒构造与Cauchy组合策略使其在广泛的椭圆对称分布中具有良好的适应性和优越的检验功效。

Abstract: We consider one-sample testing of a high-dimensional location parameter under elliptically symmetric distributions with heavy tails and pervasive cross-sectional dependence. We propose an elliptical regularized Hotelling test with Cauchy combination (ERHT--CC), based on the sample spatial median and the spatial-sign covariance matrix centered at that median. We derive its null asymptotic normality, consistent estimators of the centering and variance, and an explicit local power function. Since the power-optimal ridge parameter depends on the unknown alternative, we aggregate fixed-ridge $p$-values over a deterministic grid using the Cauchy rule. We establish a finite-grid joint Gaussian limit, justify the analytic combined $p$-value without estimating cross-ridge correlations, and characterize its local power. Simulation studies and an empirical analysis demonstrate the favorable finite-sample performance of ERHT--CC under heavy tails and pervasive dependence.

</details>

Conclusion: DM离差残差化为高维稀疏计数数据提供了一个理论上连贯、计算上高效的残差变换框架，可广泛应用于各类测序数据分析流程。

Abstract: We introduce a Dirichlet--multinomial (DM) deviance residualization for sparse, jointly overdispersed count matrices, the regime that dominates sequencing-based biochemical assays. The DM null treats each sample's count vector as a fixed-total composition with a single scalar concentration $α_0$ governing overdispersion, and arises exactly by conditioning independent negative-binomial feature counts on the observed sample total -- making the DM the joint conditional analogue of standard feature-wise overdispersed count models. The resulting transform preserves exact sparsity, evaluates in constant time per nonzero entry, agrees with multinomial residuals on singleton counts, shrinks repeated-count residuals according to the overdispersion the null tolerates, and recovers the multinomial residual as $α_0\to\infty$. The same fixed-dispersion comparison principle extends to ordered and tree-structured features via the generalized DM and the Dirichlet-tree multinomial, giving a single residual family that subsumes joint and feature-wise count nulls under a common compositional logic and is computationally lightweight enough to drop into existing sparse pipelines.

</details>

*Johannes K. Krondorfer,Christian W. Binder,Matthias Neumann,Wolfgang von der Linden*

TL;DR: SℓMC 利用对代理模型的类嵌套采样分层，将参数空间划分为信息性切片，在每个切片内从先验中抽样，实现对昂贵目标函数的分层蒙特卡洛积分，解耦了切片体积估计与目标函数评估，支持自适应方差感知的计算资源分配。

Method: 提出 Slice Monte Carlo (SℓMC) 积分方法：在廉价代理模型上运行类似嵌套采样（Nested Sampling）的过程，将参数空间划分为信息性分层（切片）；在每个切片内从先验分布生成样本；对昂贵目标函数进行分层蒙特卡洛积分估计。核心创新在于将切片体积估计与目标函数评估解耦，从而支持自适应、方差感知的计算资源分配策略。

Conclusion: SℓMC 通过代理模型引导的空间分层，实现了对昂贵积分的高效估计，其解耦设计使得计算资源可灵活分配，在贝叶斯推断场景中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Numerical integration involving expensive target functions is a common bottleneck in Bayesian inference and simulation. When a cheap surrogate is available, standard approaches such as reweighting or importance sampling often suffer from high variance and inefficient use of function evaluations. We introduce Slice Monte Carlo integration (S$\ell$MC), a method that leverages a Nested Sampling-like procedure on the surrogate to partition the space into informative strata, or $\textit{slices}$, while generating samples in the parameter space drawn from the prior within each slice. This enables stratified Monte Carlo integration of the expensive target function over the surrogate-induced partition, yielding an efficient estimate of the target integral. A key advantage of S$\ell$MC is the decoupling of slice volume estimation from target function evaluation, which allows for adaptive, variance-aware allocation of computational effort. We investigate the properties of S$\ell$MC, demonstrate how to efficiently generate posterior samples, and validate the method on simple benchmark problems.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出"学生化廉价自助法"(studentized cheap bootstrap)，通过在额外重抽样层中仅使用极少蒙特卡洛复制次数，即可达到与传统学生化自助法相同的高阶覆盖精度，并首次揭示了学生化中t分布的自由度对应于蒙特卡洛计算量而非数据量这一形式化联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出"学生化廉价自助法"，核心创新在于：(1) 将额外重抽样层的蒙特卡洛复制次数降至极少；(2) 首次形式化地建立了自助法与t分布之间的联系，指出t分布的自由度对应于额外的蒙特卡洛计算量而非数据量；(3) 通过对Edgeworth和Cornish-Fisher展开的高阶项进行显式计算与几何分析，针对极限t分布进行了定制化推导，从而保证高阶覆盖精度。

Result: 所提出的廉价自助法在仅需极少量额外蒙特卡洛复制的情况下，达到了与传统学生化自助法相同的高阶覆盖精度，显著降低了计算成本。

Abstract: The bootstrap is a versatile method for quantifying statistical uncertainty. Among its variants, a popular approach, the studentized bootstrap, provably achieves higher-order coverage error reduction compared to other benchmarks. However, its implementation typically requires an analytical form of the standard error, or otherwise an additional layer of resampling effort which can be computationally expensive. In this paper, we introduce what we call the studentized cheap bootstrap that achieves the same higher-order coverage accuracy as the conventional studentization, but substantially thinning the computational effort in the additional resampling layer to only very few Monte Carlo replications. Intriguingly, while conventional wisdom views "studentization" as an informal link between the bootstrap and t-distribution, we provide a first recognition that this link is in fact formal, notably with a distinct insight that the degree of freedom in the t-distribution corresponds to the Monte Carlo computation effort in the additional resampling layer, rather than the data size as in traditional thinking. Moreover, our desirable higher-order coverage accuracy builds crucially on this insight, as well as explicit calculations and geometric analyses of higher-order terms in the Edgeworth and Cornish-Fisher expansions tailored to limiting t-distributions.

</details>

### [44] [Systematic Exploration of 4-Expert Heterogeneous Mixture-of-Experts via Automated Pipeline Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23739)

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个自动化的异质4专家混合（MoE4）架构搜索流水线，在LEMUR神经网络数据集生态系统中运行。通过28天的搜索，生成了4,463个候选模型，发现因字母顺序枚举导致搜索空间严重偏向AirNet家族。分析揭示了覆盖偏差的根源，并提出了分层随机采样的修复方案。在偏差范围内，ShuffleNet和MobileNetV3组合产生最高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工设计异质MoE架构耗时且难以系统探索组合空间。作者希望用确定性代码生成器替代手工设计，实现大规模自动化搜索，以系统发现高性能的异质4专家MoE架构组合。

Method: 在LEMUR神经网络数据集上构建自动化搜索流水线，使用itertools.combinations按字母顺序枚举基础架构家族组合，生成MoE4集成模型。每个模型采用卷积门控网络，配合温度缩放（temperature scaling）、mixup数据增强和余弦退火学习率调度。在单块NVIDIA RTX 4090上运行28天。

Result: 共生成4,463个候选模型（197批次），1,021个评估成功。关键发现：由于字母顺序枚举，整个已探索搜索空间（理论23,751种组合的4.8%）锚定在单一家族AirNet上。在AirNet锚定范围内，ShuffleNet与MobileNetV3组合持续产出最高精度（均值准确率达0.632），FractalNet和MNASNet被识别为低产出家族，建议在后续实验中排除。

Conclusion: 该流水线揭示了确定性枚举导致的严重覆盖偏差问题，建议采用分层随机采样修复。尽管存在偏差，仍成功识别了高性能和低产出的架构家族。代码和分析工件已在NNGPT开源项目中发布。

Abstract: We present an automated large-scale search pipeline for heterogeneous 4-Expert Mixture-of-Experts (MoE4) architectures within the LEMUR neural network dataset ecosystem. Building on a hand-crafted heterogeneous MoE reference model, we replace manual design with a deterministic code-assembly generator that systematically combines base architecture families drawn from the LEMUR database into MoE4 ensembles, each governed by a convolutional gating network with temperature scaling, mixup augmentation, and cosine-annealed learning rate scheduling. Over a 28-day campaign on an NVIDIA RTX 4090, the pipeline generated 4,463 candidate models across 197 batches, of which 1,021 were evaluated successfully. A critical finding emerged from the campaign: due to alphabetical enumeration via itertools.combinations, the entire explored search space (4.8% of the theoretical 23,751 possible 4-family combinations) is anchored to a single family, AirNet. We characterise this coverage bias precisely, identify the root cause in the generator, and propose a stratified random sampling fix. Within the AirNet anchored scope, ShuffleNet and MobileNetV3 consistently co-produce the highest-accuracy ensembles (mean accuracy up to 0.632), while FractalNet and MNASNet are identified as low-yield families warranting exclusion in future campaigns. The pipeline, analysis artefacts, and corrected generator are released as part of the open-source NNGPT project at https://github.com/ABrain-One/nn-gpt

</details>


### [45] [Weight-Space Geometry of Offline Reasoning Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23740)
*Aleksandr Nikolich,Igor Kiselev,Vladimir Platonov,Karina Romanova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了六种离线强化学习/微调方法（SFT、RFT、DFT、RIFT、Offline GRPO、DPO）在推理蒸馏中的权重更新机制，发现尽管部分方法在下游准确率上接近，但它们在权重空间中的更新方向存在显著差异：SFT/RFT/RIFT几乎共线，Offline GRPO引入了较大的正交分量但仍处于同一损失盆地，而DPO则位于近乎正交的子空间，且取得了最高的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常仅以下游准确率来比较各种离线强化学习损失函数在推理蒸馏中的效果，但缺乏对这些方法在机制层面（权重更新方向）是否真正存在差异的理解。作者试图回答：这些方法产生的权重更新是机制上不同的，还是殊途同归？

Method: 以Qwen3-4B为基座模型，在相同的数学推理rollout数据上训练六种方法（SFT、RFT、DFT、RIFT、Offline GRPO、DPO），使用仅针对注意力层的LoRA。通过余弦相似度、主角度子空间分析、线性模式连通性（LMC）和中心核对齐（CKA）四种分析工具，系统比较训练后的权重增量。

Result: (i) SFT、RFT、RIFT的权重增量几乎共线（余弦相似度≥0.97，144个模块的主角度中位数约7度），GSM8K准确率接近（87-88%），McNemar检验无显著差异；(ii) DFT尽管使用相同数据，方向偏离程度超过任何基于奖励加权的方法；(iii) Offline GRPO在SFT方向上叠加了大量正交分量（全局约67%，后期层高达86%），但仍处于SFT损失盆地内；(iv) DPO位于近乎正交的子空间，存在模式连通性障碍，后期层CKA降至约0.46，但GSM8K准确率最高（93.5%），AIME26达30.0%（其他方法仅3.3-10.0%）。

Conclusion: 这些损失函数在权重空间中产生机制上不同的更新方向，而非收敛到同一点。DPO在准确率上表现最佳，但其训练使用了比其他方法小10倍的学习率，因此更新范数和准确率差距是损失函数和优化器选择的联合效应，学习率匹配的DPO对比留待未来工作。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement-learning losses (RFT, RIFT, DFT, Offline GRPO, DPO) are widely used to distill reasoning from large teachers into smaller students, and are typically compared on downstream accuracy alone. We ask whether they are mechanistically distinct or converge to a similar weight update. Training six methods (SFT, RFT, DFT, RIFT, Offline GRPO, DPO) on identical math rollouts from a single base model (Qwen3-4B) with attention-only LoRA, we analyze the resulting deltas via cosine similarity, principal-angle subspace analysis, linear mode connectivity, and CKA. We observe: (i) SFT, RFT, and RIFT have nearly colinear weight deltas (cosine >= 0.97, top-1 principal angle ~7 deg median over 144 modules) and comparable GSM8K accuracy (87-88%, n=1319; pairwise McNemar p >= 0.15); (ii) DFT diverges further in direction than any reward-weighted method despite using the same data; (iii) Offline GRPO adds a substantial component orthogonal to the SFT direction (~67% globally, up to ~86% in late layers) while staying in the SFT loss basin; (iv) DPO sits in a near-orthogonal subspace, shows a mode-connectivity barrier, and collapses late-layer CKA to ~0.46. DPO also reaches the highest accuracy in our protocol on both GSM8K (93.5%, McNemar p < 10^-9 vs. each other method) and AIME26 (30.0% vs. 3.3-10.0%); its training uses a 10x smaller learning rate than the others (the standard convention), so the update-norm and accuracy gaps reflect loss-function and optimizer choices jointly, and a learning-rate-matched DPO comparison is left for future work.

</details>

*Xianjie Guo,Yuwei Wang,Guodu Xiang,Xiaoli Tang,Kui Yu,Han Yu,Qiang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了联邦因果推理的两个核心阶段——联邦因果发现(FCD)和联邦因果推断(FCI)，通过多维度分类法组织现有方法，并首次将其形式化为统一管道中互补的阶段。FCD沿方法论范式、联邦拓扑和结构范围三轴组织，FCI按目标估计量和估计策略分类，最后讨论了隐私、通信效率等共同关切及未来挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推理对数据驱动决策至关重要，但实际数据常因隐私法规或通信约束分散于多个机构，无法集中。联邦学习虽能实现协作分析，但联邦因果发现与推断领域跨学科性强且缺乏全面综述，构成研究人员的入门障碍。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文提出多维度分类法：FCD沿三个核心设计决策轴——方法论范式（结构学习方式）、联邦拓扑（数据分区方式）和结构范围（各方获得的结构知识）；FCI按目标估计量（平均vs.个体化/条件处理效应）和估计策略（从经典加权方法到现代深度生成架构）分类。还考察了时间动态、数据异质性、缺失数据、非一致变量集等实践维度。

Result: 整理了FCD和FCI的现有方法体系，并形式化了二者的连接：FCD为FCI中有效效应估计提供所需的结构知识，构成统一联邦因果推理管道的互补阶段。同时归纳了隐私保护、通信效率、理论保证和应用领域等共同关注点。

Conclusion: 联邦因果发现与推断是互补的统一管道，当前领域面临开放的挑战，包括隐私与效用的权衡、通信效率提升、理论保证的完善以及更广泛的应用场景探索，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Causal reasoning, which encompasses the discovery of causal structures and the inference of causal effects, is fundamental to data-driven decision making. In practice, data for reliable causal analysis are often distributed across institutions and cannot be centralized due to privacy regulations or communication constraints. Federated learning (FL) addresses this by enabling collaborative analysis without raw data sharing, giving rise to the rapidly growing field of federated causal discovery (FCD) and inference (FCI). However, the interdisciplinary nature of this field and the absence of a comprehensive survey present barriers to entry for researchers. This paper bridges that gap by providing a systematic review through multi-dimensional taxonomies. Grounded in the three core design decisions underlying any FCD solution, namely how structures are learned, how data are partitioned, and what structural knowledge each party obtains, we organize FCD along three axes: methodological paradigm, federation topology, and structural scope. We further examine key practical dimensions, including temporal dynamics, data heterogeneity, missing data, and non-identical variable sets. For FCI, we categorize methods by target estimand (average versus individualized/conditional treatment effects) and by estimation strategy, from classical weighting methods to modern deep generative architectures. Unlike prior works that treat FCD and FCI separately, we formalize their connection as complementary stages of a unified federated causal reasoning pipeline, where FCD supplies the structural knowledge required for valid effect estimation in FCI. Finally, we highlight their shared concerns regarding privacy, communication efficiency, theoretical guarantees, and application domains, and conclude by identifying open challenges for future research.

</details>


### [47] [A Single Stepsize Suffices for Unprojected Linear TD(0): Simultaneous Robust and Fast Rates via Polyak--Ruppert Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24981)
*Wei-Cheng Lee,Francesco Orabona*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了马尔可夫采样下线性TD(0)算法，采用无需曲率参数先验知识的步长调度和Polyak-Ruppert平均，同时获得鲁棒的免曲率收敛速率和快速的曲率依赖收敛速率，核心工具是基于几何混合马尔可夫链的泊松方程分解技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性TD(0)算法在马尔可夫采样下的高概率分析通常需要投影步骤或依赖曲率参数ω来保证稳定性，且步长选择需要事先知道ω。本文旨在移除这些限制，设计一个无需投影、无需已知ω的单一简单步长调度，同时获得鲁棒和快速的双重收敛保证。

Method: 采用带Polyak-Ruppert平均的原始无投影TD(0)算法，步长调度为η_t ∝ 1/(τ_mix·log(t)·√t)，仅依赖混合时间τ_mix。核心技术工具是几何混合马尔可夫链的泊松方程方法：将马尔可夫噪声分解为鞅项和受控余项，在此基础上建立一种新的自界（self-bounding）归纳论证，证明迭代路径的自动一致有界性，进而建立PR平均的同时高概率收敛界。

Result: 1）所选步长保证TD(0)迭代值自动一致有界（高概率），无需投影和基于ω的稳定性论证；2）PR平均同时获得两个高概率收敛速率：鲁棒的免曲率速率 Õ(τ_mix/√T) 和快速的曲率依赖速率 Õ(τ_mix²/(ωT))，实际界取两者最小值。

Conclusion: 通过巧妙的步长设计和泊松方程分解技术，可以在马尔可夫采样下实现无投影、免曲率先验知识的线性TD(0)，同时兼有对问题条件自适应（取两速率最小值）的高概率收敛保证，为策略评估提供了更实用且理论完备的算法方案。

Abstract: We study linear TD(0) under Markovian sampling, where data are generated along a single trajectory. We provide high-probability guarantees for a plain unprojected TD(0) algorithm with Polyak-Ruppert (PR) averaging, using a single stepsize schedule $η_t \propto \frac{1}{τ_{\mathrm{mix}}\log(t)\sqrt{t}}$ that depends on the mixing time but requires no prior knowledge of the curvature parameter $ω$. Our first result shows that such a choice of the stepsize guarantees that the TD(0) iterates are automatically and uniformly bounded with high probability, without projections and without any stability argument based on $ω$. Building on this result, we establish a simultaneous high-probability convergence guarantee for the PR average: the same stepsize yields both a robust curvature-free $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\!\left(\frac{τ_{\mathrm{mix}}}{\sqrt{T}}\right)$ rate and a fast curvature-dependent $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\!\left(\frac{τ_{\mathrm{mix}}^2}{ωT}\right)$rate, with the bound taking the minimum of the two. The core technical ingredient is a Poisson-equation toolkit for geometrically mixing Markov chains, which decomposes Markov noise into a martingale term plus a controlled remainder and enables a new self-bounding inductive argument for pathwise stability.

</details>

*Ian T. Vidamour,Fernando Aguirre,Thomas J. Hayward,Matthew O. A. Ellis,Charles Swindells,Alexander McDonnell,Martin Trefzer,Finley Robins,Luca Manneschi,Susan Stepney,Tony Kenyon,Oliver J. Sutton,Jack C. Gartside,Ivan Y. Tyukin,Adnan Mehonic,Eleni Vasilaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将可训练非线性函数置于物理神经网络连接上（受KAN启发），以模拟带通滤波器在FPAA上实现。研究发现该方法的优势取决于任务类型和物理基函数的平滑性：在平滑连续值目标（如机器人运动学、连续控制、光伏MPPT）上比MLP更参数高效，但在分类决策边界上无优势。网络可迁移至硬件，约35,000个连接，CMOS实现预计功耗约30微瓦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理神经网络架构大多将非线性器件响应强制用作标量权重，未能充分利用物理器件的非线性计算潜力。作者希望探索将可训练非线性函数置于连接上是否能带来参数效率优势，并探究这种优势的来源与条件。

Method: 受Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）启发，在物理神经网络的每条连接上放置可训练的非线性函数（而非标量权重），在FPAA上以模拟带通滤波器实现这些函数。通过对比在不同任务类型（平滑连续值目标 vs. 分类决策边界）上的表现，系统评估参数效率。还进行了硬件迁移验证（约35,000连接）、CMOS功耗预估（~30μW），以及忆阻器仿真复现。

Result: 1. 在平滑连续值目标（机器人运动学、连续控制、光伏最大功率点跟踪）上，所需节点和连接数远少于MLP；2. 在分类类决策边界任务上无参数效率优势；3. 训练后的网络可高保真迁移至硬件（约35,000连接）；4. 专用CMOS实现预估功耗约30μW；5. 忆阻器仿真可复现相同行为，表明优势源于连接上可训练非线性本身，而非特定器件。

Conclusion: 将可训练非线性置于物理连接的策略在平滑连续函数拟合任务上具有显著的参数效率优势，且该优势具有器件无关性。这为低功耗物理神经网络设计提供了新的范式——通过在连接而非节点上放置非线性计算元素，实现更高效的模拟机器学习硬件。

Abstract: Physical neural networks promise low-power machine learning by computing directly with analogue device physics, but most architectures force nonlinear device responses to act as scalar weights. Inspired by Kolmogorov-Arnold networks, we place trainable nonlinear functions on the connections, making each physical connection a learnable computational element. Realising these functions as analogue band-pass filters on field-programmable analogue arrays, we find that the benefit is task-dependent and follows from the smoothness of the physical basis: the networks represent smooth, continuously valued targets, including robotic kinematics, continuous control, and photovoltaic maximum-power-point tracking, with far fewer nodes and connections than multilayer perceptrons, but offer no parameter-efficiency advantage on classification-like decision boundaries. Trained networks transfer to hardware across approximately 35,000 connections with quantified fidelity, and a dedicated CMOS implementation is projected to operate at approximately 30 microwatts. A memristive realisation reproduces the same behaviour in simulation, indicating that the advantage comes from placing trainable nonlinearity on connections, rather than from a particular device.

</details>


### [49] [Latent Block-Diffusion Temporal Point Processes: A Semi-Autoregressive Framework for Asynchronous Event Sequence Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24982)
*Shuai Zhang,Yancheng Chen,Chuan Zhou,Yang Liu,Xixun Lin,Xiangyu Zhao,Jun Zhu,Zhi-Ming Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种半自回归的时间点过程框架LBDTPP，通过在潜空间中将事件序列分块，并在每个块内进行高斯扩散，实现了可变长度、高质量的事件序列生成，平衡了自回归的长度灵活性与扩散模型的并行生成优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归TPP方法在多步生成中存在误差累积问题，而非自回归扩散方法通常受限于固定长度输出。需要一种既能生成变长序列又能保证高质量并行的新框架。

Method: 提出Latent Block-Diffusion Temporal Point Processes (LBDTPP)：在潜空间中对事件块定义自回归概率分布，在每个块内部执行高斯扩散；通过顺序生成块并同时采样块内事件，实现半自回归生成。理论上推导了Wasserstein误差界，证明块级生成相比事件级自回归生成可减少误差累积。

Result: 在六个真实世界基准数据集上，LBDTPP在无条件和条件生成任务中均优于最先进的TPP基线方法。消融实验验证了潜空间扩散和块级生成的优势，并揭示了生成质量与块大小之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: LBDTPP有效结合了自回归与扩散模型的优势，在变长事件序列生成任务中实现了高质量与灵活性的统一，代码已开源。

Abstract: Modeling and sampling from the underlying distribution of asynchronous event sequences are crucial in various real-world applications, including social networks, medical diagnosis, and financial transactions. Existing autoregressive methods suffer from error accumulation during multi-step generation, while non-autoregressive diffusion methods are typically limited to fixed-length output sequences. In this paper, we propose Latent Block-Diffusion Temporal Point Processes (LBDTPP), a novel semi-autoregressive TPP framework that introduces a latent block diffusion mechanism for high-quality and variable-length event sequence generation. The core idea is to define an autoregressive probability distribution over event blocks in latent space and perform Gaussian diffusion within each block. By sequentially generating blocks while simultaneously sampling events in each block, LBDTPP preserves the length flexibility of autoregressive TPPs and inherits the parallel high-quality generation capability of diffusion models. Theoretically, we derive Wasserstein error bounds showing that, under suitable local approximation and prefix-stability assumptions, block-wise generation can reduce error accumulation compared with event-wise autoregressive generation. Extensive experiments on six real-world benchmark datasets demonstrate that LBDTPP outperforms state-of-the-art TPP baselines in both unconditional and conditional generation tasks. Further empirical analyses verify the benefits of latent-space diffusion and block-wise generation, and reveal the trade-off between generation quality and block size. Our code is available at https://github.com/Zh-Shuai/LBDTPP.

</details>


### [50] [Synergizing Physically Constrained MCMC and Chemical-Informed Gaussian Processes for Reaction Network Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23757)
*Runzhe Liu,Zihao Wang,Wenbo Yang,Shengyang Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PC-MCMC-CIGP灰盒工作流，整合spike-and-slab拓扑采样、物理约束筛选和化学信息高斯过程残差模型，在H₂+Br₂和苯乙烯环氧化反应上验证了其在动力学方程发现和贝叶斯优化中的有效性，并系统比较了多种采集函数的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏、含噪声的化学时间序列数据中提取可解释的控制方程极具挑战，因为离散反应拓扑与连续动力学参数高度耦合，现有方法难以将两者解耦并进行物理一致的推断。

Method: 提出PC-MCMC-CIGP工作流：(1) spike-and-slab先验的MCMC拓扑采样实现反应网络结构推断；(2) 硬守恒约束和热力学筛选保证物理可行性；(3) 化学信息高斯过程(CIGP)对残余动力学进行建模，用于参数校准；(4) 显式的不确定性感知采集策略(PC-EI, EI, GWU, 不确定性采样, discrepancy hunting等)指导实验设计。

Result: 在H₂+Br₂基准上，约束采样器能区分基元自由基路径与欺骗性唯象拟合；在苯乙烯环氧化反应中，CIGP优化循环较GP-BO基线将最终产率提升12.5%；10种子采集研究表明PC-EI显著减少低产率建议，EI类准则在最终产率上表现最强。

Conclusion: 将化学领域知识(守恒定律、热力学)嵌入贝叶斯推断和优化框架，能够在数据稀疏时实现更可靠的反应网络发现和更高效的实验设计；PC-EI提供了安全探索与高产率优化的良好折中。

Abstract: Extracting interpretable governing equations from sparse, noisy chemical time-series data remains difficult because discrete reaction topology and continuous kinetic parameters are tightly coupled. We present PC-MCMC-CIGP, a reproducible gray-box workflow that combines spike-and-slab topology sampling, hard conservation and thermodynamic screening, and a Chemical-Informed Gaussian Process (CIGP) residual model for parameter calibration and experimental design. The methodological contribution is not a new MCMC or GP family in isolation; rather, it is the integration of these components into a physically constrained workflow with explicit uncertainty-aware acquisition choices. On the H2 + Br2 benchmark, the constrained sampler distinguishes elementary radical pathways from deceptive phenomenological fits in our experiments. On styrene epoxidation, the CIGP optimization loop improves final yield by 12.5% over the reported GP-BO baseline. A new 10-seed acquisition study shows that EI, GWU, PC-EI, uncertainty sampling, discrepancy hunting, and random search have different trade-offs: PC-EI substantially reduces low-yield BO suggestions, while EI-style criteria give the strongest final-yield performance.

</details>


### [51] [Exploring Dualistic Meta-Learning to Enhance Domain Generalization in Open Set Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23758)
*Xiran Wang,Jian Zhang,Lei Qi,Yang Gao,Yinghuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MEDIC，一种双元学习策略，通过同时考虑域间和类间任务分割的隐式梯度匹配，解决开放集域泛化中因正负样本不平衡导致的决策边界偏移问题，在开放集和封闭集场景均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域泛化通常假设源域和目标域标签空间一致，忽视了标签不匹配的现实情况。开放集域泛化旨在未见过域中识别未见过类别，但传统的一对多分类器方法存在严重的正负样本不平衡问题：正样本稀少、负样本众多，导致决策边界向正类偏移，模型过度拒绝来自已知类别的分布外数据。本文旨在找到对域和类别均平衡的最优决策边界。

Method: 提出双元学习与联合域-类匹配策略（MEDIC）。核心思想是通过元学习框架，同时对域间任务分割和类间任务分割进行隐式梯度匹配，使模型在优化过程中兼顾域不变性和类别判别性，从而学习到对域和类别双重平衡的决策边界。

Result: 实验结果表明，MEDIC在开放集场景中显著优于现有方法，同时在封闭集域泛化任务上保持有竞争力的泛化能力。

Conclusion: MEDIC通过双元学习联合域-类匹配，有效解决了开放集域泛化中正负样本不平衡导致的过拒绝问题，实现了开放集与封闭集兼顾的良好性能。

Abstract: Domain generalization learns from multiple source domains to generalize to unseen target domains. However, it often neglects the realistic case of label mismatch between source and target. Open set domain generalization is then proposed to recognize unseen classes in unseen domains. A simple approach trains one-vs-all classifiers to separate each class and detect outliers as unknown. Yet, the imbalance between few positive samples and many negative samples skews the decision boundary towards the positive ones, leading the model to over-reject out-of-distribution data, even from known classes in unseen domains. In this paper, we propose a novel meta-learning stategy called dualistic MEta-learning with joint DomaIn-Class matching (MEDIC), which considers implicit gradient matching towards inter-domain and inter-class task splits simultaneously to find optimal boundaries balanced for both domains and classes. Experimental results show that MEDIC not only outperforms prior methods in open set scenarios, but also maintains competitive close set generalization ability.

</details>


### [52] [Training for the Model You Return: Improving Optimization for Iterate-Averaged Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25086)
*Kwok Chun Au,Adam Block*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE，一个围绕AdamW的轻量包装器，通过将当前权重拉向其指数移动平均来改进返回的迭代平均模型的性能。该方法源自最优控制框架，在二次模型中可严格提升极限均方误差，并在1-2B参数LM的监督微调和GPT-2预训练中一致优于AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型pipeline通常返回训练迭代的指数移动平均（EMA）而非最终迭代本身。这引出一个根本性问题：既然最终返回的是迭代平均，应该如何调整训练过程以提升该平均的性能？现有优化器设计未考虑这一"返回平均"的最终目标，存在理论与实践之间的差距。

Method: 将面向迭代平均估计器的优化器设计形式化为最优控制问题。在连续时间随机二次模型中，求解最小化返回平均误差（加上干预惩罚）的控制策略。该策略的实用近似导出PACE算法：在AdamW基础上，用一个带裁剪的逐坐标控制强度将当前权重拉向EMA方向。作者还证明了PACE的简化版本在标准随机凸优化速率下收敛，且在二次设定下可严格改进极限均方误差，改进倍数可任意大。

Result: 理论上证明PACE的收敛性和在二次模型中相比标准EMA的严格优势。实验上，在1-2B参数LM的监督微调（SFT）和GPT-2在FineWeb上的预训练中，PACE在广泛的learning rate、decay schedule等超参数设置下均优于AdamW及EMA-evaluated AdamW。

Conclusion: PACE作为一种轻量、即插即用的AdamW包装器，通过最优控制视角解决了"为迭代平均而优化"的问题，在理论和实验上均展现出对现有方法的改进，尤其适用于大规模语言模型训练中常使用EMA返回值的场景。

Abstract: Many modern Language Model (LM) pipelines return an averaged model, such as an exponential moving average of the training iterates, rather than the final iterate itself. This raises a fundamental question: given that we will return an iterate average, how should we change training to improve the performance of this average? We study this question by formulating optimizer design for the iterate-average estimator as an optimal-control problem. In a continuous-time stochastic quadratic model, we solve for the control strategy that minimizes the error of the returned average subject to a penalty on the size of the intervention. A practical approximation to this controller yields PACE, a lightweight wrapper around AdamW that pulls the live weights toward their exponential moving average with a clipped, per-coordinate control strength. We prove that a stylized version of PACE converges at the standard stochastic convex optimization rate, up to a factor depending on the averaging rule, while in the quadratic setting it can strictly improve the limiting squared error of the iterate-average estimator and can do so by an arbitrarily large factor on some instances. Empirically, our results suggest that PACE improves over AdamW and EMA-evaluated AdamW in supervised fine-tuning of 1-2B parameter LMs and in GPT-2 pretraining on FineWeb for a wide range of learning rates, decay schedules, and other hyperparameters.

</details>


### [53] [One Ruler: A Same-Hands Re-Evaluation of Bivariate Causal Direction on Tuebingen, with a Parameter-Free Compression Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23767)
*Wietse Stienstra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出因果发现领域普遍存在的基准测试不可比较问题，通过对图宾根因果对的统一重新评估，揭示已发表方法的高性能很大程度上源于测试集选择和不强制决策等协议差异。作者提出一个零参数的压缩基线方法，在统一标准下达到与最优方法相当的性能，从而质疑了该领域声称的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图宾根因果对数据集上的准确率被广泛用于比较不同因果发现方法，但每篇论文使用不同的数据子集、加权方式、模型选择和决策率，导致结果不可比。作者认为需要一种"同一双手"的统一重新评估，以公平比较各方法的真实性能。

Method: 作者提出三项核心举措：(1) 统一协议：在完全相同的102对数据上运行所有方法，强制每对给出决策，不允许调参；(2) 引入极简基线：排序条件压缩（sorted-conditional compression），将量化、排序、一阶差分后的数据输入现成压缩器bz2，零拟合参数；(3) 系统性地重现文献中报告的结果，追溯已发布数据与实际性能之间的差异来源。

Result: 基线压缩方法达到74.7%加权准确率（p=3.7e-7），在SLOPE评估的100对上得76.0%，仅比SLOPE的强制决策结果（77.2%）低1.2个百分点（McNemar p=0.39）。RECI的忠实重跑得70.7%，而非通常引用的77.5%（追溯至复制粘贴错误）。SLOPE发表的82.4%是选择性决策的结果。统一标准下所有方法集中在70-75%区间，零参数压缩器与最强方法持平。

Conclusion: 已发表的高准确率主要由测试集模型选择和显著性门控弃权两类机制虚增。压缩分数本身可作为模型无关的混淆标志。作者通过预注册的证伪测试进一步限定了该方法理论解释的边界，并公开了代码、预注册文件和逐对输出结果。

Abstract: Headline accuracies on the Tuebingen cause-effect pairs are routinely compared across papers even though each is measured under its authors' own protocol -- different pair subsets, weightings, model-selection, and decision rates. We argue this is the wrong comparison and run the right one: a same-hands re-evaluation in which every method is run by us on the identical 102 pairs, with one strict rule -- no tuning and a decision forced on every pair. As a clean reference point we introduce a deliberately minimal baseline: sorted-conditional compression, which feeds quantized, sorted, first-differenced data to an off-the-shelf compressor (bz2) and has zero fitted parameters. Under the common ruler the ranking differs sharply from the literature. Our baseline reaches 74.7% weighted accuracy (p = 3.7e-7); on the same 100 pairs that SLOPE is evaluated on it scores 76.0%, a 1.2-point gap below the authors' own forced-decision SLOPE (77.2%) that is well inside noise (McNemar p = 0.39). A faithful re-run of RECI lands at 70.7% -- inside the original authors' reported error bar, not the 77.5% often quoted (which we trace to a mis-copied cell). SLOPE's published 82.4% is a decided-subset figure: scoring the authors' own stored output only on the pairs its significance test chose to answer reproduces 81.7%. Under the common ruler the methods cluster in the low-to-mid 70s and the zero-parameter compressor ties the strongest of them. We document the mechanisms that inflate published figures (test-set model selection, significance-gated abstention) and contribute two further results: compression score magnitude is a model-free confounding flag (p = 2.8e-68), and a pre-registered falsification test fails in an instructive way that bounds the method's theoretical interpretation. Code, pre-registrations, and per-pair outputs are released.

</details>


### [54] [Efficient Adaptive Data Acquisition via Pretrained Belief Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.25197)
*Daolang Huang,Zhuoyue Huang,Conor Hassan,Luigi Acerbi,Samuel Kaski,Tom Rainforth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: POLAR提出了一种将信念表示与策略学习解耦的框架：利用预训练预测基础模型作为信念状态编码器，在其上训练策略头，统一处理贝叶斯实验设计、贝叶斯优化和主动学习等自适应数据采集任务，在样本效率和性能上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应数据采集方法存在两难：基于后验的方法依赖可能错误设定或有偏的代理模型与后验近似；直接策略学习方法仅从历史观测映射，未能利用可用的模型表示，导致学习困难。需要一个既能利用模型表示、又能避免后验近似偏差的统一框架。

Method: POLAR基于"最优数据采集仅通过充分信念状态依赖观测历史"的洞察，将表示学习与策略学习解耦：(1) 使用预训练的预测基础模型作为信念状态编码器，提取历史观测的充分表示；(2) 在该表示之上训练一个轻量策略头；(3) 通过仅改变任务特定的效用函数来训练策略，统一适用于贝叶斯实验设计、贝叶斯优化和主动学习三种场景。

Result: 实验表明，POLAR在多种任务上均优于最先进的摊销方法，且所需的训练样本数量显著更少，在摊销数据采集的可扩展性和效率方面实现了重要提升。

Conclusion: POLAR通过解耦表示学习与策略学习，展示了预训练基础模型在自适应数据采集中的巨大潜力，为构建更高效、更通用的摊销数据采集方法开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Learning effective policies for adaptive data acquisition remains challenging: posterior-based methods rely on surrogate models and posterior approximations that can be misspecified or biased, while direct policy-learning methods map from historical observations and fail to exploit available model representations, making learning harder. We introduce policy learning with belief representations (POLAR), based on the insight that optimal data acquisition depends on the observation history only through a sufficient belief state. Specifically, POLAR decouples representation learning from policy learning by leveraging pretrained predictive foundation models as belief-state encoders, training a policy head on top of their representations. This yields a simple, unified amortised policy learning framework for Bayesian experimental design, Bayesian optimisation, and active learning, differing only in the task-specific utility used to train the policy. Empirically, we find that POLAR outperforms state-of-the-art amortised methods across diverse tasks while requiring far fewer training samples, demonstrating a significant step in the scalability and efficiency of amortised data acquisition.

</details>


### [55] [Deciphering Fingerprints of 3D Molecular Surfaces for Accurate Epitope Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23830)
*Fang Wu,Weihao Xuan,Jure Leskovec,Yejin Choi,Li Erran Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SurfBind 是一种基于分子表面表征的构象表位预测框架，采用Transformer架构，通过patch级表面建模、结合物感知交叉注意力和层次化粗到细预测范式，在SAbDab和DB5.5基准上取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表位预测方法主要依赖序列或骨架结构信息，难以捕捉非连续性、由表面驱动的构象表位。因此，需要一种直接在分子表面层面进行操作的方法，以更好地建模抗体-抗原识别的几何和物理化学模式。

Method: SurfBind在分子表面表征上直接运行，核心包括：(1) patch级表面建模，将分子表面划分为局部patch并编码几何与物理化学特征；(2) 基于Transformer的架构，整合结合物感知交叉注意力机制，实现抗体-抗原表面之间的信息交互；(3) 层次化粗到细预测范式，先进行粗略区域定位再进行精细残基级表位预测。

Result: 在SAbDab和DB5.5等表位识别基准数据集上，SurfBind取得了state-of-the-art性能，并在未见抗体和不同构象状态下展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: SurfBind证明了交互感知的表面建模对于理解蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用的关键机制具有重要价值，为构象表位预测提供了一种有效的新范式。

Abstract: Molecular surfaces encode the geometric and physicochemical patterns that determine antibody-antigen recognition, central to epitope prediction. However, existing methods rely on sequences or backbone structures and struggle to capture discontinuous, surface-driven epitopes. This study presents SurfBind, a surface-centric learning framework for epitope prediction that operates directly on molecular surface representations. SurfBind integrates geometric and physicochemical cues through a Transformer-based architecture with patch-level surface modeling, binder-aware cross-attention, and a hierarchical coarse-to-fine prediction paradigm. Experiments on challenging epitope identification benchmarks, including SAbDab and DB5.5, demonstrate that SurfBind achieves state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization across unseen antibodies and conformational states, highlighting the value of interaction-aware surface modeling for understanding the crucial mechanisms of protein-protein interactions.

</details>


### [56] [Reconstructing GRACE Terrestrial Water Storage with Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks: An Application to South America](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23833)
*Lukas Arzoumanidis,Lara Johannsen,Klara Middendorf,Annette Eicker,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图神经网络（MTGNN）的深度学习应用，利用ERA5气象强迫数据将GRACE陆地水储量异常（TWSA）重建延伸至1940年，在流域尺度达到0.94的相关性，性能与现有方法相当但仅需约一半到十分之一的预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRACE/GRACE-FO卫星提供了唯一直接、全球一致的TWS变化观测，但记录始于2002年，对于气候尺度分析而言时间过短。需要一种可靠的方法将TWS记录回推至卫星时代之前。

Method: 采用多变量时间序列图神经网络（MTGNN）架构，将原用于城市交通预测的模型适配到卫星大地测量任务。通过静态混合邻接矩阵编码空间依赖，该矩阵结合了测地距离与气候时间序列的滞后相关性，以捕捉局地水文耦合和大尺度遥相关。输入为ERA5日尺度降水、蒸散发和径流数据，输出为月尺度GRACE-like TWSA。

Result: 重建结果网格单元Pearson相关系数达0.69，流域平均相关系数达0.94，偏差接近零。成功再现了2015/16年厄尔尼诺和2020/21年拉尼娜事件的空间指纹。与GTWS-MLrec、RM-REC、GRAiCE等现有方法系统比较表明，图模型在流域尺度上具有统计竞争力，相关性在最佳基线0.025以内，但仅使用约一半到十分之一的预测因子。同时揭示了所有模型在干旱地区的共同弱点。

Conclusion: 图神经网络方法能够以更精简的预测因子集合实现与现有复杂重建方法相当甚至接近的性能，验证了图结构在编码水文空间依赖关系方面的有效性。代码已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Terrestrial water storage (TWS) integrates snow, soil moisture, surface water, and groundwater and is a key indicator of how climate variability and human activity reshape the global water cycle. The GRACE and GRACE-FO satellite missions provide the only direct, globally consistent observations of TWS change, but their record only begins in 2002 which is too short for many climate-scale analyses. We present a deep learning application that reconstructs monthly GRACE-like TWS anomalies (TWSA) back to 1940 by learning the relationship between daily ERA5 meteorological forcing (precipitation, evapotranspiration, runoff) and monthly GRACE observations. In contrast to prior reconstruction approaches based on grid-cell-wise regression, CNNs, or LSTMs, we adapt a multi-variate time series graph neural network (MTGNN) architecture, which was originally developed for mobility and traffic forecasting on urban sensor networks to this satellite-geodesy task. Spatial dependencies are encoded in a static, interpretable hybrid adjacency matrix that combines geodesic proximity with lagged correlations of climatic time series, capturing both local hydrological coupling and large-scale teleconnections. The reconstruction achieves a grid-cell Pearson correlation of 0.69, a basin-mean correlation of 0.94, and a near-zero bias, and it reproduces the spatial fingerprints of the 2015/16 El Niño and 2020/21 La Niña events. A systematic comparison with established reconstruction approaches (GTWS-MLrec, RM-REC, GRAiCE) shows that the graph-based model is statistically competitive at basin scale, reaching a correlation within 0.025 of the best baseline while using only roughly half to a tenth of the predictors the other models require and revealing characteristic weaknesses in arid regions in all models. The complete implementation is publicly available at github.com/hcu-cml/MTGNN-TWS-Reconstruction-GRACE

</details>


### [57] [The Degeneracy Distillery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23838)
*T. Lucas Makinen,Deaglan J. Bartlett,Niall Jeffrey,Benjamin D. Wandelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“简并蒸馏器”（degeneracy distillery）方法，通过估计并扁平化Fisher信息矩阵，自动且符号化地检测和解析参数-数据对中的简并参数组合。该方法从信息几何角度刻画简并性，无需实际观测数据，能发现使参数对数据产生独立效应的符号坐标变换，在保持后验估计校准度的同时可减少高达10倍的仿真预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当模型中的多个参数或标签产生相似数据时，即出现“简并”（degeneracy），这使得标签预测和逆问题求解变得困难。现有方法难以自动、全局且符号化地识别和消解这类简并性，因此需要一种仅基于参数-数据对即可检测并解析简并组合的新方法。

Method: 提出“简并蒸馏器”方法，核心步骤如下：(1) 从参数-数据（或参数-仿真）对中估计Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）；(2) 通过探索似然的信息几何，对FIM进行扁平化处理，检测简并方向；(3) 自动发现符号化的坐标变换，将简并参数组合转化为对数据产生独立效应的新坐标。该方法将简并性视为物理模型的内禀属性，无需实际观测数据，且能在期望意义上全局扁平化Fisher信息，而非仅在单点处扁平化。

Result: 在多个合成和真实世界问题上验证了该方法。结果表明：发现的符号坐标变换能有效识别产生独立效应的参数组合；相比于仅在单点扁平化的后验方法，该方法实现了Fisher信息的全局扁平化；在下游神经后验估计中，仿真预算最多可减少10倍，同时保持验证校准度匹配，并额外获得对系统的物理洞察。

Conclusion: 简并蒸馏器能够自动、符号化地检测并解析物理模型中的参数简并性，显著降低下游后验估计的仿真成本，同时提供可解释的物理洞察，在计算效率与科学理解两方面均具优势。

Abstract: When two or more parameters or labels produce similar data, they are degenerate, or hard to distinguish. Degeneracies render both label prediction and inverse problems difficult, since both machine learning algorithms and probabilistic samplers rely on the distinguishability of data and its gradients with respect to parameters. However, identifying degeneracies in physical models or real-world datasets can be elucidating about the choice of model or the underlying process that produces the data. We present the degeneracy distillery, a method that (1) detects and (2) resolves degenerate parameter combinations (a) automatically and (b) symbolically, from parameter-data (or parameter-simulation) pairs alone, through estimation and flattening of the Fisher information matrix. By exploring the information geometry of the likelihood, we characterize degeneracies as an intrinsic property of the physical model, requiring no realised data observation. We demonstrate our approach on a range of synthetic and real-world problems, discovering symbolic coordinate transformations that identify the combinations of parameters of a model which yield independent effects on the data. The resulting coordinates flatten the Fisher information in expectation globally, in contrast to posterior-based methods that flatten only at a single point, and substantially reduce the simulation budget required for downstream neural posterior estimation. In test cases we require up to $10\times$ fewer simulations for posterior estimation at matched validation calibration whilst simultaneously gaining physical insight on the system.

</details>


### [58] [Machine Learning Modeling for Real-Time Melt Pool Monitoring in Laser Powder Bed Fusion Additive Manufacturing: A Hybrid Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23851)
*Inioluwa Emmanuel,Zhuo Yang,Ho Yeung,Xinyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI/ML在激光粉末床熔融增材制造中的实时熔池监测应用，提出了一种EfficientNetB0特征嵌入与随机森林结合的混合分类框架。在镍基超合金625的1200张熔池图像数据集上，该混合方法取得了0.9458的准确率和仅1.15毫秒的单张推理时间，显著优于纯深度学习模型，证明在数据有限的增材制造环境中，预训练卷积特征与传统集成方法的结合可提供稳健且计算高效的实时异常检测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光粉末床熔融（LPBF）增材制造对实时过程监测有迫切需求，但实际工厂环境中存在两个核心约束：(1) 可获取的训练数据通常有限；(2) 开放架构LPBF机器的硬件资源受限，对模型推理延迟和计算开销有严格要求。现有纯深度学习方法在推理速度和精度之间难以平衡，因此需要一种兼顾高精度与低延迟的轻量化异常检测方案。

Method: 作者提出了一个二元图像分类框架：首先收集NIST AMMT平台上镍基超合金625的1200张熔池图像（正常/异常均衡分布），按80/20划分训练-测试集并进一步90/10划分验证集，经标准化缩放、归一化和标签保持的数据增强处理。基准测试涵盖三种迁移学习架构（ResNet50、EfficientNetB0、MobileNetV2）与两种随机森林方法：一种基于EfficientNetB0特征嵌入的混合方法，一种基于原始像素特征的基线方法。评估指标包括准确率、精确率、召回率、F1分数、AUC，以及训练时间、推理延迟和CPU/GPU占用率。

Result: 混合EfficientNetB0+随机森林方法在测试集上表现最优：F1=0.9451，准确率=0.9458，AUC=0.9904，单张推理仅1.15毫秒。相比之下，纯深度学习模型推理时间显著更高而精度更低。该方法在实现亚毫秒级推理的同时保持了高检测精度。

Conclusion: 预训练卷积特征与经典集成学习方法的结合，为数据有限的增材制造环境提供了一条稳健且计算高效的实时熔池异常检测路径，很好满足了工厂车间监控对精度与部署便捷性的双重需求。

Abstract: This work investigates the implementation of artificial intelligence and machine learning (AI/ML) for real-time monitoring in laser powder bed fusion (LPBF) additive manufacturing. We developed a binary image classification framework for distinguishing normal and abnormal melt pool images using a balanced dataset of 1,200 images collected from Nickel superalloy 625 on the NIST AMMT platform. The study evaluates accuracy and inference time based on control requirements and hardware limitations of open-architecture LPBF machines. We benchmark three transfer learning architectures (ResNet50, EfficientNetB0, and MobileNetV2) against two Random Forest approaches: one trained on EfficientNetB0 feature embeddings (hybrid) and one trained on raw pixel features (baseline). Images are stratified into 80/20 train-test splits, with a further 90/10 validation split on the training set, and undergo standardized resizing, normalization, and label-preserving data augmentation to emulate realistic process variability. Each model is evaluated using accuracy, precision, recall, F1 score, and area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC), along with training time, inference latency, and CPU & GPU usage to capture deployability constraints relevant to factory-floor monitoring. The hybrid EfficientNetB0-plus-Random Forest approach achieves the best performance on the held-out test set, with an F1 score of 0.9451, accuracy of 0.9458, and AUC of 0.9904, while maintaining sub-millisecond per-image inference (1.15 ms). In contrast, purely deep learning models exhibit significantly higher inference times with lower accuracy. These results demonstrate that combining pre-trained convolutional features with classical ensemble methods provides a robust, computationally efficient route to real-time melt pool anomaly detection in data-limited additive manufacturing environments.

</details>


### [59] [Sesame: Structure-Aware Molecular Generation via Spatial Density-Map Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23856)
*Konstantin Yatsenko,Arvind Thiagarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sesame 是一种基于扩散的分子生成模型，通过新型空间对偶模块（spatial pairformer）将部分分子结构和蛋白口袋表示为连续空间密度图作为条件，同时支持从头生成和基于片段的先导化合物优化，并引入原子类型、键类型和位置的联合去噪扩散框架及轨迹微调方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前药物设计领域的生成式分子模型需要同时理解小分子结构、蛋白-配体相互作用，并具备从头生成能力。然而，从化学家提供的部分结构（如骨架或片段）出发进行分子生长——即先导化合物优化的核心操作——这一关键能力常被忽视。现有模型缺乏统一的机制来同时支持从头生成和片段条件化的先导优化。

Method: 1. 提出空间对偶模块（spatial pairformer），将部分分子结构和周围蛋白口袋统一表示为连续空间密度图作为条件输入；2. 构建扩散框架，对原子类型、键类型和三维坐标进行联合去噪生成；3. 引入轨迹微调（trajectory finetuning）方案，在模型自身采样轨迹上训练以提升生成质量；4. 在配体-only和蛋白-配体数据集上进行大规模训练。

Result: 该单一条件机制能够同时支持从头分子生成和片段条件化的先导化合物优化，使药物化学家可将命中化合物修剪为骨架后由 Sesame 进行有效生长。轨迹微调进一步提升了生成分子的质量。

Conclusion: Sesame 通过统一的连续空间密度图条件机制，将从头生成和先导优化整合于同一扩散框架中，为计算药物设计提供了一个兼具灵活性和实用性的分子生成平台。

Abstract: Generative molecular models for drug design are a promising direction with much active research. In the next phase of computational drug design, such models will need to understand small molecule structure and protein-ligand interactions, and they will need to possess the machinery to generate molecules de novo. Incorporating each feature poses a critical challenge. Equally important, yet often treated as secondary, is the ability to grow a molecule from a partial starting point -- a scaffold or fragment supplied by a chemist -- which is the central operation of lead optimization. We present Sesame (Spatial Evoformer for a Structure-Aware Molecular Engine), a diffusion-based molecular generation model that leverages a novel spatial pairformer module to condition on partial molecular structure and the surrounding protein pocket, both expressed as continuous spatial density maps. This single conditioning mechanism supports both de novo generation and fragment-conditioned lead optimization, letting a medicinal chemist prune a hit to a scaffold and have Sesame grow it in productive ways. In addition to this module, we also introduce a diffusion framework for joint denoising of atom types, bond types, and positions, along with a trajectory finetuning scheme that trains on the model's own sampling rollouts to improve generation quality. Sesame is trained on a large corpus of ligand-only and protein-ligand datasets.

</details>


### [60] [Are Safety Guarantees in Neural Networks Safe? How to Compute Trustworthy Robustness Certifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23858)
*Merkouris Papamichail,Konstantinos Varsos,Giorgos Flouris,João Marques-Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对神经网络鲁棒性认证问题，提出"apothem度量"替代传统体积最大化目标，证明可在O(n)次验证器调用内计算apothem最优认证，同时证明体积最优认证在预言机模型下不可行。此外引入对偶认证概念，并实现ParallelepipedoNN系统，在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上实现最小边长至少两倍提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒性认证方法追求最大化轴对齐超矩形的体积，但近期计算复杂性结果证明体积最优认证在合理时间内不可计算。因此需要寻找一个既具有理论可解性、又能有效衡量认证质量的替代优化目标。

Method: 1. 引入apothem度量——超矩形中心到各面最短距离，即最小半边长；2. 设计算法，通过线性次数的神经网络验证器（预言机）调用来计算apothem最优认证；3. 从理论上证明，即使忽略预言机调用代价，也不存在体积最优的预言机算法；4. 提出对偶认证概念，为类别所有实例提供一个区间上界；5. 实现ParallelepipedoNN系统进行实验验证。

Result: 1. 理论上证明apothem最优认证可在输入域直径相关的线性次验证器调用内完成；2. 证明了体积最优的预言机算法根本不存在（即使忽略预言机成本）；3. 在MNIST和Fashion MNIST基准上的实验表明，相比现有方法，最小边长至少提升两倍。

Conclusion: Apothem度量是鲁棒性认证中兼具计算可行性与实用价值的优化目标，ParallelepipedoNN系统在标准数据集上显著超越现有工作，为AI安全中的对抗样本防御提供了新的理论和实践基础。

Abstract: A primary challenge in AI safety is the existence of adversarial examples -- slightly distorted inputs that cause a neural network (NN) to misclassify. To mitigate this problem, recent research focuses on the computation of robustness certifications, which, for a given input, determine the largest distortion the input may receive without breaking the network's prediction. Robustness certifications can be interpreted as an axis-aligned hyper-rectangle (multi-dimensional intervals). Most existing approaches focus on maximizing the certification's volume, but recent intractability results prohibit the computation of volume-optimal certifications in reasonable time. We introduce the apothem measure and show how to compute apothem-optimal certifications in a linear number of calls to a NN verifier (oracle) w.r.t. the input domain's diameter. Moreover, we prove that we cannot have a volume-optimal, oracle-based algorithm, even if we discard the oracle costs. Also, we introduce dual certifications -- an interval including all instances of a class -- thus providing apothem-minimum upper bounds to a robustness certification. Further, we present the ParallelepipedoNN system, which we evaluate on the standard MNIST and Fashion MNIST benchmarks. A preliminary comparison with existing work on the same datasets reveals at least two-fold improvement w.r.t. the minimum edge length.

</details>


### [61] [Exact Schur-Sylvester Dimensionality Reductions for Non-Smooth Stochastic Complexity and Manifold Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23867)
*Trenton Lau,Gary P. T. Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用分块Schur补和Sylvester行列式恒等式的新方法，将NML编码长度计算中投影算子和体积因子的复杂度从O(N³)降至O(k³+N²k)，在保持双精度数值等价的前提下实现超14,100倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则化非光滑估计器（如Lasso）的NML精确编码长度计算长期受制于几何投影与体积积分中O(N³)的立方级复杂度瓶颈，使得大规模统计推断不可行。

Method: 利用分块Schur补将(N+k)×(N+k)广义KKT矩阵的投影求逆转化为低维运算；采用Sylvester行列式恒等式将(N-k)×(N-k) Gram矩阵的行列式计算化简；并将该降复杂度策略推广至稀疏SVM、Elastic Net和Group Lasso。

Result: 复杂度从O(N³)降至O(k³+N²k)每步；高维数据集实证基准测试显示恒定超过14,100倍加速，同时保持双精度数值等价性；通过ESS/秒指标验证了采样器效率。

Conclusion: 该精确等价降复杂度方案使大规模非光滑NML估计变得高度可行，为高维统计推断开辟了新路径。

Abstract: The exact computation of the Normalized Maximum Likelihood (NML) codelength for regular non-smooth estimators (e.g., Lasso) has been historically limited by the cubic scaling walls of manifold-constrained projection and volume integration. At each step of the geometric Propose-and-Project Metropolis--Hastings (PPMH) sampler, evaluating the projection operator requires inverting an $(N+k) \times (N+k)$ generalized KKT matrix, while calculating the volume factor requires the determinant of an $(N-k) \times (N-k)$ Gram matrix. This paper presents an exact, mathematically equivalent formulation that bypasses both bottlenecks by utilizing the block Schur complement and Sylvester's determinant identity. We prove that the computational complexity of both operations collapses from $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$ to $\mathcal{O}(k^3 + N^2 k)$ per step. We generalize this reduction to Sparse Support Vector Machines (SVMs), Elastic Net, and Group Lasso. Finally, we provide a rigorous numerical stability analysis and evaluate the sampler's efficiency using the Effective Sample Size (ESS) per second. Our empirical benchmarks on high-dimensional datasets confirm a constant speedup exceeding $14{,}100\times$ while maintaining double-precision numerical equivalence, rendering exact non-smooth NML estimation highly tractable for large-scale statistical inference.

</details>


### [62] [Federated Survival Analysis in Healthcare: A Multi-Model Evaluation on Cross-Institutional Heterogeneous Breast Cancer Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23871)
*Natalia Moreno-Blasco,Anusha Ihalapathirana,Pekka Siirtola,Miguel Fernandez-de-Retana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对联邦生存分析进行了系统性的多模型评估，在三类代表性模型（Cox PH、DeepSurv、RSF）和三种联邦优化策略（FedAvg、FedProx、FedAdam）下，基于真实跨机构乳腺癌队列，比较了集中式、本地和联邦训练的性能，并给出了面向实际部署的决策指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存分析依赖大规模多样化队列，但单机构数据有限且隐私法规限制数据集中化。联邦学习虽可保护隐私，但在真实异构条件下的联邦生存建模效果尚缺乏系统理解，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 在自然异构分布的跨机构乳腺癌队列上，系统比较三种生存模型（Cox比例风险模型、DeepSurv、随机生存森林RSF）在集中式、本地和联邦三种训练范式下的表现；对基于梯度的模型（Cox PH、DeepSurv）额外评估FedAvg、FedProx、FedAdam三种联邦优化策略。

Result: 联邦学习一致优于本地训练，性能接近甚至偶尔超越集中式训练；RSF在区分度、校准度和跨异构客户端的鲁棒性方面整体最优；联邦性能依赖于客户端分布的多样性；FedAvg和FedProx比FedAdam更稳定、更强。

Conclusion: 基于实验结果，给出了面向实践的决策导向指南，将数据、隐私、可解释性和资源约束映射到推荐的模型和训练范式选择，为医疗领域联邦生存建模的落地提供了明确参考。

Abstract: Survival analysis is central to clinical decision-making, yet reliable time-to-event models require large, diverse cohorts that are rarely available at a single institution, while privacy regulations restrict the centralization of patient data. Federated learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving alternative by training shared models without exchanging raw data, but its effectiveness for survival modeling under realistic, heterogeneous conditions remains insufficiently understood. This paper presents a systematic, multi-model evaluation of federated survival analysis on a cross-institutional breast cancer cohort with naturally heterogeneous distributed clients. Three representative survival models, the Cox Proportional Hazards model, DeepSurv, and Random Survival Forest (RSF), are compared across centralized, local, and federated training, and three federated optimization strategies (FedAvg, FedProx, and FedAdam) are assessed for the gradient-based models. Results show that FL consistently outperforms local training and approaches, and occasionally exceeds, centralized performance, while RSF offers the best overall balance of discrimination, calibration, and robustness across heterogeneous clients. We further find that performance depends on the diversity of client distributions, and that FedAvg and FedProx are stronger and more stable than FedAdam. Based on these findings, we derive practical, decision-oriented guidelines mapping data, privacy, interpretability, and resource constraints to recommended model and training-paradigm choices for federated survival modeling in healthcare.

</details>


### [63] [MGI: Member vs Generated Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23872)
*Bihe Zhao,Michel Meintz,Juangui Xu,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"成员 vs 生成推理"（MGI）这一新任务，指出已有的成员推理和归因方法在此任务上系统性地失败，并提出了三阶段的 Data Circuit Breaker（DCB）方法，通过结合自编码器和潜在生成器的互补信号来有效区分训练成员与生成样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成模型能够生成与人类创作内容难以区分的样本，且可能记忆并复现训练数据。这引出了一个关键问题：给定一个样本，它究竟是模型的真实训练数据，还是模型自身生成的？现有的成员推理方法会系统性地将生成样本误判为训练成员，而归因方法则常将训练成员误判为生成样本，二者均因依赖似然相关信号而失效。因此需要一种能有效区分二者的新方法。

Method: 提出 Data Circuit Breaker（DCB），一种三阶段方法：(1) 利用生成模型的自编码器（autoencoder）提取重建相关信号；(2) 利用潜在生成器（latent generator）提取生成相关信号；(3) 将两类互补信号进行融合，以区分训练成员与模型生成样本。该方法在图像自回归模型和扩散模型上进行了验证，并能应对模型衍生（model derivative）等挑战性场景。

Result: DCB 在多个生成模型上一致地克服了成员推理和归因方法的不足；即使模型生成了训练样本的近复本，DCB 仍然有效；在基于生成数据训练新模型的模型衍生场景下，DCB 也表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: MGI 是一个重要但未被充分研究的问题。现有方法由于过度依赖似然信号而在此任务上失效。DCB 通过融合自编码器和潜在生成器的互补信号，提供了一种鲁棒且通用的解决方案，在多种模型和复杂场景下均表现优异。

Abstract: As generative models increasingly produce samples that are indistinguishable from human-created content, it becomes difficult to determine whether a given data point was part of a model's natural training set or was generated by the model itself, especially when models memorize and reproduce training data. We formalize this challenge as Member vs Generated Inference (MGI): given a sample and a target generative model, infer whether the sample is a true training member or a generated output of that model. Focusing on image generation, we show that existing membership inference methods systematically misclassify generated samples as training members, while attribution-based methods often misclassify true members as generated. This failure arises because both approaches rely on likelihood-related signals that are similarly elevated for training examples and for the model's own outputs. To address MGI, we propose Data Circuit Breaker (DCB), a three-stage method that combines complementary signals from a generative model's autoencoder and latent generator to distinguish training members from generated samples. Across multiple generative models, including image autoregressive and diffusion models, DCB consistently addresses the shortcomings of membership inference and attribution methods, remains effective even when models reproduce near-duplicates of training samples, and generalizes to challenging model derivative settings in which new models are trained on generated data.

</details>


### [64] [GRACE: Gated Refinement for Accurate Causal Edge Discovery in High-Dimensional Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23880)
*Mohammad Fesanghary,Abhinav Havaldar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRACE 提出一种基于 Hard Concrete 门控与 $L_0$ 正则化的因果边精细化方法：先用快速线性条件独立性检验构建高召回骨架，再用单一门控模型剔除假阳性边，实现可扩展的非线性因果发现，在合成基准和真实河流数据上均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代时间序列因果发现面临两难：基于约束的非线性条件独立性检验计算代价过高、无法扩展；基于分数的方法避免了检验，但需任意阈值对连续边分数二值化，导致分数分布重叠、决策不鲁棒。本文旨在结合两者优势，用可微门控机制实现无需阈值、计算高效且统计可靠的大规模因果发现。

Method: GRACE 分两阶段：(1) 使用快速线性条件独立性检验（如偏相关）构建高召回率的因果骨架图；(2) 训练单一门控预测模型，每个候选边对应一个独立的 Hard Concrete 门控参数，结合 $L_0$ 正则化使门控值自动趋向 0 或 1 的双峰分布，直接从门控值得到二值化因果边决策。正则化强度根据问题维度和骨架密度自适应调整。还提出时序自助抽样（temporal bootstrap）变体以应对分布偏移和混淆因子。

Result: 在多种合成图拓扑（无标度、ER随机、小世界）及高达 $d=100$ 维度的基准测试中，GRACE 相比基础 CI 方法显著提升 F1，且保持高精度，优于基于注意力和基于分数的方法；匹配或超越昂贵非线性 CI 检验的性能，速度提升 75 倍。在易北河流域真实流量数据上，temporal bootstrap 变体恢复 11 条真实因果边中的 9 条，仅 1 个假阳性（F1=0.86，AUROC=0.99），将骨架中 106 个假阳性减少 99%。

Conclusion: GRACE 提供了一种可扩展、无需阈值、计算高效的因果发现框架，通过 $L_0$ 门控精细化使二值决策清晰鲁棒，自适应正则化消除了超参数调节负担，在大规模时间序列和存在分布偏移的实测数据中均展现出优异的因果发现能力。

Abstract: From climate teleconnections to gene regulation, modern time-series datasets encompass tens or hundreds of interacting variables, making causal discovery increasingly challenging. Constraint-based methods offer statistical rigor but their nonlinear CI tests are infeasible at scale, while score-based alternatives avoid CI testing but require arbitrary thresholds to binarize continuous edge scores. We propose GRACE ($\textbf{G}$ated $\textbf{R}$efinement for $\textbf{A}$ccurate $\textbf{C}$ausal $\textbf{E}$dge discovery), which refines constraint-based discovery using Hard Concrete gates with $L_0$ regularization: each candidate edge has an independent gate whose values concentrate near 0 or 1, yielding a clean bimodal separation that makes the binary decision robust, unlike the narrow, overlapping score distributions produced by $L_1$ and attention-based methods. A fast linear CI skeleton provides high-recall candidates; a single gated model then prunes false positives by learning which edges genuinely improve prediction, with automatic regularization adapted to problem dimensions and skeleton density. Systematic experiments on synthetic benchmarks, spanning diverse graph topologies (scale-free, Erdős-R'enyi, small-world) and dimensionalities up to $d=100$, show that GRACE substantially improves F1 over its base CI method while maintaining high precision, and outperforms attention-based and score-based alternatives. GRACE matches or exceeds expensive nonlinear CI tests at a fraction of the cost ($75\times$ faster). On a real-world river flow dataset, where rainfall confounders, variable propagation lags, and distributional shifts violate standard assumptions, a temporal bootstrap variant of GRACE recovers 9 of 11 causal edges along the Elbe River with only 1 false positive ($F_1 = 0.86$, AUROC${} = 0.99$), reducing the skeleton's 106 false positives by 99%.

</details>


### [65] [ARIA: Adaptive Region-Based Importance Allocation for Conditional Diffusion Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23898)
*Loay Mualem,Vinh Tong,Samir Darouich,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARIA 提出一种自适应分配训练资源的方法，在条件扩散模型的知识蒸馏中，基于教师-学生模型在各条件区域上的在线差异估计，动态聚焦训练于对齐不足的区域，从而提升蒸馏效率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件扩散模型的蒸馏需要覆盖广泛的条件空间，但实际中配对数据有限，且合成图像成本高昂。现有方法（如RC）通过切换条件扩展了条件空间，但未解决训练资源如何在不同条件区域间高效分配的问题。

Method: 提出 ARIA（Adaptive Resource allocation In conditional diffusion distillation）框架：将条件空间划分为粗粒度区域，在线维护各区域的教师-学生差异估计（通过追踪机制），据此自适应分配训练更新，使蒸馏集中在差异持续存在的区域，同时保持原始蒸馏目标不变。理论分析表明，在有界方差和漂移假设下，该追踪机制能有效跟踪训练过程中的差异变化。

Result: ARIA 在多数架构和设置下优于 RC（Random Conditioning），尤其在未见过的和代表性不足的条件区域上增益最为显著。

Conclusion: ARIA 通过在线差异追踪实现条件空间上的自适应训练分配，有效提升了条件扩散模型蒸馏的效率与泛化性能，为大规模条件空间下的知识蒸馏提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Distilling conditional diffusion models aims to transfer the behavior of a large teacher to a smaller student while preserving alignment across conditioning inputs. Unlike recognition tasks, knowledge distillation in conditional diffusion often struggles to transfer knowledge beyond the training distribution, since the predicted noise strongly depends on the conditioning signal. As a result, effective distillation requires exploring a large conditioning space. In practical settings, this creates a major bottleneck. Paired image-condition data may be limited, and generating synthetic images for every available condition is often computationally infeasible, while the pool of conditions, such as text prompts, can be extremely large. Recent work addresses this issue by switching conditions during training, exposing the student to a broader conditioning space without changing the distillation objective. Yet this raises a complementary question: once a large conditioning corpus is available, how should the training effort be allocated? In this work, we introduce ARIA, a framework that adaptively allocates training effort across coarse regions of the conditioning space. By maintaining online estimates of teacher-student discrepancy at the region level, ARIA focuses updates where misalignment persists while preserving the original distillation objective. Empirically, ARIA improves over RC across most architectures and settings, with the clearest gains observed in unseen and underrepresented regimes. We also provide a theoretical analysis showing that the proposed tracking mechanism follows the evolving discrepancy during training under bounded variance and drift assumptions.

</details>


### [66] [Closing the Loop: Formally Verified Law as a Reward Signal for Self-Improving Legal AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23913)
*Armin Heydari,Torben Leowald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将数学AI中的"大语言模型提议、验证器裁决"范式适配到法律领域的架构，通过LLM驱动的自动形式化、扩展自Catala的形式法律演算验证内核以及基于形式证明轨迹的解释生成，为法律AI训练提供可形式验证的奖励信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律AI面临核心挑战：法律推理中计算性部分需要可证明正确性，而开放纹理的法律分析缺乏可靠的结构性保障。传统强化学习在缺乏确定性外部验证器的法律领域存在反馈闭环缺口。本文旨在将数学AI中成功的"提议-验证"范式迁移至法律领域。

Method: 提出三层架构：(1) LLM驱动的自动形式化，将法律文本转化为扩展Catala的形式法律演算；(2) 验证内核，对形式化后的法律推理进行确定性验证；(3) 基于形式证明轨迹的解释生成。该架构对法律的计算性部分提供可证明正确性，对开放纹理分析提供结构性保障（确保每个论证阶段被处理、论证在正确阶段进行、步骤间演绎链接有效）。在德国法程序期限计算、美国宪法商业条款分析、跨法域制裁比例原则三个场景上进行了验证演示。

Result: 架构成功在三个不同法域和法律领域（德国程序法、美国宪法、跨法域比例原则）上演示了可行性。确定性外部验证器能为法律问题提供可验证结果，从而弥合法律领域强化学习的传统反馈闭环缺口，在结构上有利于法律AI训练。

Conclusion: 将"LLM提议、验证器裁决"范式从数学AI适配到法律领域是可行的。该架构不仅为法律的计算性部分提供可证明正确性、为开放纹理分析提供结构性保障，还为法律AI训练提供了关键的确定性验证信号，解决了法律领域强化学习缺乏可靠外部反馈的根本性问题。

Abstract: This article develops an architecture that creates a formally verifiable reward signal to train legal AI, adapting the LLM proposes, verifier disposes paradigm from mathematical AI to the distinctive demands of law. We present an architecture comprising LLM-driven autoformalization into a formal legal calculus extending Catala, a verification kernel, and explanation generation grounded in formal proof traces. For the computational components of law, the architecture provides provable correctness. For open-textured legal analysis, it provides structural guarantees: every required stage of the legal argument is addressed, argumentation is exercised at the correct stages and not omitted, and the deductive links between steps are valid. We demonstrate the architecture on procedural deadline calculations in German law, Commerce Clause analysis in U.S. constitutional law, and cross-jurisdictional sanction proportionality. We further show that the same architecture has a structural advantage for legal AI training: a deterministic external verifier supplies verifiable outcomes for legal problems and thereby closes the traditional reinforcement-learning loop gap in law.

</details>


### [67] [Catastrophic Compositional Generation: Why Vanilla Diffusion Models Fail to Extrapolate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23920)
*Duncan Soiffer,Chandler Squires,Yuan Guan,Jason Hartford,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了在组合生成任务中，当目标分布相对于源分布为分布外时，vanilla条件扩散模型在推理阶段无法有效生成样本，评分估计误差比推理近似误差更具灾难性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对条件扩散模型在组合生成任务中的根本局限性进行理论分析，质疑现有推理阶段技术（如Feynman-Kac修正）能否真正解决由分布外目标分布引发的问题。

Method: 采用理论引导的泛化论证与精心设计的实验相结合：通过合成数据与真实数据上的实验，分别考察推理阶段近似误差与评分估计误差对组合生成性能的影响。

Result: 发现当目标分布与源分布构成分布外关系时，评分估计误差对性能的破坏远大于推理近似误差；Feynman-Kac修正等方法仅能降低后者，无法根本解决问题。

Conclusion: 推断在特定合理设定下，没有任何推理阶段技术能高效地从目标分布采样，暗示组合生成任务需要完全不同的方法论路径。

Abstract: The task of compositional generation involves using a conditional generative model, trained only on a subset of the possible conditions, to produce samples from compositionally-defined target distributions such as a geometric combination of the source distributions. In this work, we argue that this task is often infeasible for vanilla conditional diffusion models: we conjecture that no inference-time technique can efficiently produce samples from the target distribution in certain well-motivated settings. This idea is supported by theory-guided generalization arguments and carefully-designed experiments on both synthetic and realistic data. In particular, while recent methods such as Feynman-Kac correction reduce inference-time approximation error, our results show that score estimation error has a more catastrophic effect on performance when the target distribution is out-of-distribution with respect to the sources, highlighting the need for a different approach to this task.

</details>


### [68] [KLip-PPO: A per-sample KL perspective on PPO-Clip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23932)
*Riccardo Colletti,Robin Holzinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PPO-Clip的截断代理目标梯度与一个逐样本KL惩罚代理目标完全等价，该KL系数的表达式可闭合地写为重要性比率和优势函数的函数。这一等价关系在MuJoCo连续控制基准上得到验证，揭示PPO-Clip的隐式惩罚是一个阶跃函数，为算法推广提供了新的设计轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO在文献中以两种形式被呈现——截断代理目标和KL惩罚，二者被视为具有不同梯度、不同超参数和不同参考实现的独立算法。大量实证工作对二者进行比较，但二者之间是否存在深层理论联系尚不清楚。作者旨在揭示这两种表面上不同的形式之间的精确等价关系。

Method: 作者通过梯度分析，证明截断代理目标的梯度可以被一个逐样本KL代理目标精确复现，其中KL惩罚系数是重要性比率和优势函数的闭合形式函数。该恒等式在每一个minibatch步骤和整个内循环中成立。理论推导后，在五个MuJoCo连续控制基准上进行实验验证，对比两种损失的训练曲线。

Result: 截断代理目标的梯度等价于一个KL代理目标的梯度，其逐样本系数为β_t = |A_t|·𝟙[sign(A_t)·(ρ_t−1) ≥ ϵ, sign(A_t)·A_t·(ρ_t−1) ≥ 0] / KL[π_old||π_new]。在MuJoCo基准上，两种损失产生不可区分的训练曲线。PPO-Clip的隐式逐样本惩罚在信任域边界处呈现阶跃函数形态。

Conclusion: PPO的两种形式并非独立算法，截断代理目标本质上是一种隐式的、逐样本KL惩罚，其惩罚系数呈阶跃函数形态。这一结构特征为沿系数"形状"维度设计和推广PPO算法开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) is the standard policy-gradient algorithm for on-policy reinforcement learning. The literature presents it in two forms, a clipped surrogate that bounds the importance ratio between successive policies and a Kullback-Leibler penalty between them. These forms are treated as separate algorithms with their own gradients, their own hyperparameters, and their own reference implementations, and a sizeable body of empirical work compares them. We show that the gradient of the clipped surrogate is reproduced exactly by a Kullback-Leibler surrogate whose coefficient varies per sample, with closed-form dependence on the importance ratio and the advantage. The identity holds at every minibatch step and across the entire inner loop, and on five MuJoCo continuous-control benchmarks the two losses produce indistinguishable training curves. The reformulation exposes a structural feature of the clipped surrogate that the min notation hides. PPO-Clip's implicit per-sample penalty is a step function at the boundary of the trust region, and the shape of this coefficient is the natural design axis for generalising the algorithm. We sketch the resulting follow-up directions in the discussion.

</details>


### [69] [DREG: A Layer-Wise Jacobian Regularization as a General-Purpose Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23942)
*Rowan Martnishn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过对960个实验的大规模实证研究，系统评估了导数正则化惩罚(DREG)的效果。结果表明DREG在总体准确率和干净数据环境下排名第一，在噪声鲁棒性上仅次于谱归一化；在GELU激活函数下表现最佳；在数据稀缺场景下优势最为显著。DREG使用单一固定超参数即可工作，可作为即插即用的正则化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏对导数正则化(DREG)的孤立、大规模实证研究，不清楚DREG在什么条件（激活函数、数据集类型、数据量）下有效、何时失效，以及它相较于其他正则化器的相对优势。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统回答DREG"何时、何地、为何"有效。

Method: 设计了一个全交叉因子实验：4种激活函数 × 6种正则化器 × 8个数据集 × 5个随机种子，共960个实验。6种正则化器包括DREG、Weight Decay、IGPen、Spectral Normalization等。DREG统一使用固定超参数λ=10^{-2.5}，不做逐数据集调参。采用Wilcoxon符号秩检验进行统计显著性分析。

Result: 三项核心发现：(1)DREG在所有正则化器中总体准确率最高，显著优于无正则化基线、Weight Decay和IGPen(Wilcoxon p≤0.031)，噪声鲁棒性仅次于谱归一化；(2)DREG在GELU激活函数下全局最优，尤其在混乱视觉和NLP基准上表现突出；(3)数据稀缺时DREG优势最明显，验证其作为几何归纳偏置可替代数据量的正则化效应。

Conclusion: DREG是一种有效的即插即用正则化器，其设计原理——将正则化压力集中在激活导数最大的层而非均匀约束网络——与其在GELU激活、数据稀缺场景下的卓越表现一致，对现代Transformer架构的深度学习具有直接应用价值。

Abstract: We present a large-scale empirical study isolating the contributions of the Derivative Regularization penalty (DREG). Across a fully-crossed factorial sweep of 960 experiments spanning 4 activations, 6 regularizers, 8 datasets, and 5 random seeds, we ask: when, where, and why does DREG work? Our results establish three principal findings. First, DREG achieves the highest overall and clean-regime accuracy among all regularizers evaluated (significantly so against the unregularized baseline, Weight Decay, and IGPen; Wilcoxon $p \leq 0.031$). It ranks second in noise robustness behind Spectral Normalization (SN) - the only two layer-wise regularizers in the study. Second, DREG is globally the best-performing regularizer under GELU, the default activation in modern transformer architectures, particularly on both messy vision and messy NLP benchmarks, suggesting direct applicability to frontier deep learning settings. Third, DREG's advantage over competing regularizers is most pronounced under data scarcity, consistent with its role as a geometric inductive bias that substitutes for the regularizing effect of data volume. Throughout, DREG is applied with a single fixed hyperparameter $λ= 10^{-2.5}$ and no per-dataset tuning, supporting its characterization as a plug-and-play regularizer for neural networks with nontrivial Jacobian structure. These findings are consistent with DREG's design: concentrating regularization pressure on layers where the activation derivative is largest, rather than constraining the network uniformly.

</details>


### [70] [Learning the Koopman Operator using Attention Free Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23957)
*Mohammed Nagdi,Evangelos-Marios Nikolados,Alexey Yermakov,Mars Gao,Nathan Kutz,Filippo Menolascina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补组件来增强Koopman自编码器的长期预测鲁棒性：注意力无关的潜在记忆模块(AFT)和动态重编码机制，有效抑制了长时域预测中的误差发散和流形漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman自编码器虽然能在潜在空间实现线性预测，但在处理具有切换、连续谱或强瞬态特性的系统时，长时域rollout会偏离学习到的流形，导致相位和幅度误差累积。现有注意力机制（如多头注意力）虽然能捕捉时序依赖，但计算复杂度高。因此需要一种既高效又能抑制误差发散的方法。

Method: 提出两个互补组件：(1) **AFT (Attention-Free Latent Memory) 模块**：聚合过去一小段窗口的潜在变量，在每次Koopman更新前生成修正后的潜在表示。采用无注意力机制设计，线性时间复杂度，仅增加约30k参数（$3d^2 + T^2$），少于同等容量的多头注意力。(2) **动态重编码**：通过轻量级在线变点检测触发器（EWMA、CUSUM及序贯双样本检验）检测潜在空间漂移，将偏离的预测投影回自编码器流形。

Result: 在三个基准系统（Duffing振子、Repressilator、IRMA）上，所提模型相较于Koopman自编码器和同等容量的多头注意力模型，持续降低了误差累积。与GRU和Transformer自编码器相比（均从初始条件及50步上下文条件下评估），Koopman+AFT（可选加重编码）在长时域误差更低的同时保持更低的推理延迟。在长达1000步的预测时域上均报告了改进，并进行了触发策略的消融实验。

Conclusion: Koopman+AFT（配合可选重编码）构成了一种快速、紧凑的预测器，能够在长时域上稳定地保持在学习的流形上，有效解决了Koopman自编码器的误差漂移问题。
